肌内免疫球蛋白治疗基孔肯雅热后慢性关节痛的疗效评估

Evaluation of the Efficacy of Intramuscular Immunoglobulin in Treating Post-Chikungunya Arthralgia
作者： Suraj Sudarshan Doshi, Santosh Borkar, Shubham Sanap, Shivraj Konde, Rhythm Tahilramaney, Vipul Pise, Animesh Gunjal
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)感染可能继发持续性肌肉骨骼症状,特别是慢性关节痛和关节炎,导致疼痛、影响活动能力和功能,并降低生活质量。常规治疗主要为对症治疗,可能包括止痛药、非甾体抗炎药(NSAIDs)、糖皮质激素、选择性改善病情抗风湿药(DMARDs)和康复治疗,这些治疗通常缓解效果不完全,并与长期不良反应相关。目前支持针对基孔肯雅热相关慢性关节痛特定治疗的证据仍有限。由于其免疫调节特性,肌内免疫球蛋白(IMIG)可能代表一种潜在的治疗选择。本研究旨在评估肌内免疫球蛋白治疗与基孔肯雅热后慢性关节痛患者疼痛、关节痛严重程度和关节活动度变化之间的关联,并记录与治疗相关的不良反应。这项前瞻性观察性研究在一家三级医院内进行了18个月。纳入了60名持续至少一个月的基孔肯雅热后慢性关节痛成人患者。肌内免疫球蛋白作为临床管理的一部分被给予,并前瞻性地观察治疗前后结果。未给予其他试验性药物。疼痛强度使用视觉模拟量表(VAS)进行评估,关节痛严重程度使用患者报告的关节痛库存表(PRAI)进行评估,关节活动度通过临床活动范围评估在基线、一周、一个月和六个月时进行测量。使用了描述性统计、配对比较、重复测量方差分析和多变量回归分析。VAS平均值从基线的7.6 ± 1.1降至一周的4.9 ± 1.2、一个月的3.1 ± 1.0和六个月的2.5 ± 0.8(p < 0.001)。PRAI评分从基线的6.2 ± 1.0降至一周的3.8 ± 1.1、一个月的2.4 ± 0.9和六个月的1.9 ± 0.7(p < 0.001)。在基线时,70.0%的参与者有中度至重度的关节活动受限;在六个月时,80.0%的参与者关节活动度恢复正常或接近正常。85.0%的参与者报告了总体功能改善。六名患者(10.0%)出现了轻度注射部位疼痛,四名(6.7%)出现了短暂的低热;未观察到严重不良事件或过敏反应。在多变量分析中,基线VAS值较高与更大的疼痛缓解相关,而年龄增加和慢性关节痛持续时间较长则与较小的缓解相关。在本前瞻性观察性研究中,肌内免疫球蛋白的使用与疼痛、关节痛严重程度和关节活动度的改善相关。需要受控的随机研究来确立其疗效、比较效果和安全性。所提供的证据未报告超过六个月的长期随访。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)感染可能继发持续性肌肉骨骼症状,特别是慢性关节痛和关节炎,导致疼痛、影响活动能力和功能,并降低生活质量。常规治疗主要为对症治疗,可能包括止痛药、非甾体抗炎药(NSAIDs)、糖皮质激素、选择性改善病情抗风湿药(DMARDs)和康复治疗,这些治疗通常缓解效果不完全,并与长期不良反应相关。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性研究在一家三级医院内进行了18个月。纳入了60名持续至少一个月的基孔肯雅热后慢性关节痛成人患者。
核心方法
疼痛强度使用视觉模拟量表(VAS)进行评估,关节痛严重程度使用患者报告的关节痛库存表(PRAI)进行评估,关节活动度通过临床活动范围评估在基线、一周、一个月和六个月时进行测量。使用了描述性统计、配对比较、重复测量方差分析和多变量回归分析。
主要结果
VAS平均值从基线的7.6 ± 1.1降至一周的4.9 ± 1.2、一个月的3.1 ± 1.0和六个月的2.5 ± 0.8(p < 0.001)。PRAI评分从基线的6.2 ± 1.0降至一周的3.8 ± 1.1、一个月的2.4 ± 0.9和六个月的1.9 ± 0.7(p < 0.001)。在多变量分析中,基线VAS值较高与更大的疼痛缓解相关,而年龄增加和慢性关节痛持续时间较长则与较小的缓解相关。
解释与创新
在本前瞻性观察性研究中,肌内免疫球蛋白的使用与疼痛、关节痛严重程度和关节活动度的改善相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,肌内免疫球蛋白可能是一种管理基孔肯雅热后慢性关节痛的有前景的治疗选择,可能比常规治疗提供更好的症状缓解并伴随较少的长期不良反应。
局限与证据强度
需要受控的随机研究来确立其疗效、比较效果和安全性。所提供的证据未报告超过六个月的长期随访。

Evaluation of the Efficacy of Intramuscular Immunoglobulin in Treating Post-Chikungunya Arthralgia

Authors: Suraj Sudarshan Doshi, Santosh Borkar, Shubham Sanap, Shivraj Konde, Rhythm Tahilramaney, Vipul Pise, Animesh Gunjal
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) infection may be followed by persistent musculoskeletal symptoms, particularly chronic arthralgia and arthritis, causing pain, impairing mobility and function, and reducing quality of life. Conventional management is primarily symptomaticnd may include analgesics, non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs (NSAIDs), corticosteroids, selected disease-modifying antirheumatic drugs (DMARDs), and rehabilitation, which often provide incomplete relief and are associated with long-term adverse effects. The evidence supporting specific therapies for persistent chikungunya-related rheumatic disease remains limited. Intramuscular immunoglobulin (IMIG), because of its immunomodulatory properties, may represent a potential therapeutic option. To evaluate the association of intramuscular immunoglobulin therapy with changes in pain, joint arthralgia severity, and joint mobility in patients with post-chikungunya arthralgia, and to document treatment-related adverse effects. This prospective observational study was conducted in a tertiary care hospital over 18 months. Sixty adults with post-chikungunya arthralgia of at least one-month duration were included. IMIG was administered as part of clinical management, and outcomes were prospectively observed before and after treatment. No additional investigational drug was administered. Pain intensity was assessed using the Visual Analogue Scale (VAS), arthralgia severity using the Patient-Reported Arthralgia Inventory (PRAI), and joint mobility by clinical range-of-motion assessment at baseline, one week, one month, and six months. Descriptive statistics, paired comparisons, repeated-measures ANOVA, and multivariable regression were used. Mean VAS decreased from 7.6 ± 1.1 at baseline to 4.9 ± 1.2 at one week, 3.1 ± 1.0 at one month, and 2.5 ± 0.8 at six months (p < 0.001). Mean PRAI score decreased from 6.2 ± 1.0 at baseline to 3.8 ± 1.1 at one week, 2.4 ± 0.9 at one month, and 1.9 ± 0.7 at six months (p < 0.001). At baseline, 70.0% of participants had moderate-to-severe restrictions of joint movement; at six months, 80.0% had normal or near-normal mobility. Overall functional improvement was reported by 85.0% of participants. Mild injection-site pain occurred in six patients (10.0%) and transient low-grade fever in four (6.7%); no serious adverse events or allergic reactions were observed. In multivariable analysis, a higher baseline VAS was associated with greater pain reduction, whereas increasing age and longer duration of arthralgia were associated with smaller reductions. IMIG administration was associated with improvements in pain, arthralgia severity, and joint mobility in this prospective observational cohort. Controlled randomized studies are required to establish efficacy, comparative effectiveness, and safety.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) infection may be followed by persistent musculoskeletal symptoms, particularly chronic arthralgia and arthritis, causing pain, impairing mobility and function, and reducing quality of life. Conventional management is primarily symptomatic and may include analgesics, non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs (NSAIDs), corticosteroids, selected disease-modifying antirheumatic drugs (DMARDs), and rehabilitation, which often provide incomplete relief and are associated with long-term adverse effects.
Design and population
This prospective observational study was conducted in a tertiary care hospital over 18 months. Sixty adults with post-chikungunya arthralgia of at least one-month duration were included.
Core methods
Pain intensity was assessed using the Visual Analogue Scale (VAS), arthralgia severity using the Patient-Reported Arthralgia Inventory (PRAI), and joint mobility by clinical range-of-motion assessment at baseline, one week, one month, and six months. Descriptive statistics, paired comparisons, repeated-measures ANOVA, and multivariable regression were used.
Main results
Mean VAS decreased from 7.6 ± 1.1 at baseline to 4.9 ± 1.2 at one week, 3.1 ± 1.0 at one month, and 2.5 ± 0.8 at six months (p < 0.001). Mean PRAI score decreased from 6.2 ± 1.0 at baseline to 3.8 ± 1.1 at one week, 2.4 ± 0.9 at one month, and 1.9 ± 0.7 at six months (p < 0.001). In multivariable analysis, a higher baseline VAS was associated with greater pain reduction, whereas increasing age and longer duration of arthralgia were associated with smaller reductions.
Interpretation and novelty
IMIG administration was associated with improvements in pain, arthralgia severity, and joint mobility in this prospective observational cohort.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that intramuscular immunoglobulin may be a promising therapeutic option for managing post-chikungunya arthralgia, potentially offering better symptom relief with fewer long-term adverse effects compared to conventional treatments.
Limitations and evidence strength
Controlled randomized studies are required to establish efficacy, comparative effectiveness, and safety. The supplied evidence does not report long-term follow-up beyond six months.

扎克病毒和基孔肯雅病毒在伊朗西南部查巴哈尔市的血清流行病学研究-2025

Seroepidemiology of dengue and chikungunya viruses in Chabahar city, southwestern Iran-2025
作者： ‪Mostafa Salehi-Vaziri, Tahmineh Jalali, Sana Eybpoosh, Laya Farhan Asadi, Mahsa Tavakoli, Zahra Hosseini, Farnoosh Arbabi, Seyed Marzieh Sajadi, Ehsan Sheykh Noori, Faramarz Mobaraki
摘要中文翻译摘要 扎克病毒和基孔肯雅病毒(DENV、CHIKV)通过伊蚊传播,在热带和亚热带地区构成了重要的公共卫生挑战。本研究调查了DENV和CHIKV在查巴哈尔市的血清流行率,该市自2023年以来已建立伊蚊Aedes aegypti,并报告了本地DENV的血清流行率。2025年1月至2月期间,从该市八个卫生中心的服务区域共收集了650份血清样本,并使用ELISA试剂盒检测DENV和CHIKV IgG。总体ELISA定义的DENV IgG血清流行率为14.3%(95% CI 11.7–17.3%),而CHIKV为0.9%(95% CI 0.4–2.1%)。DENV的最高血清流行率出现在Zibashahr和urban-2区的城区(25%),最低则出现在Kambel Soleiman(4%)。男性DENV血清流行率显著高于女性(17% vs. 10.8%,p = 0.012),且随着教育水平的提高,血清流行率下降(p = 0.021)。年龄较大与DENV血清流行率增加相关(p = 0.040)。职业与DENV血清流行率无显著关联。未发现蚊虫叮咬报告与血清流行率之间存在显著关联。这些发现表明,在查巴哈尔市的总体人群中存在ELISA定义的DENV IgG血清流行率,提示之前曾暴露于DENV。某些地区较高的血清流行率强调了在高风险群体和高密度伊蚊区域加强监测和针对性控制措施的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查扎克病毒和基孔肯雅病毒在伊朗西南部查巴哈尔市的血清流行率,以应对这些病毒在热带和亚热带地区带来的公共卫生挑战。由于报告了本地DENV的血清流行率以及基孔肯雅病毒在相同环境中的传播潜力,本研究显得尤为重要。
设计与对象
本研究于2025年1月至2月在伊朗西南部的查巴哈尔市进行,涉及从八个卫生中心服务区域收集的650份血清样本。研究人群包括来自不同地区和人口统计学特征的个体,重点关注总体人群。
核心方法
血清样本使用ELISA试剂盒检测DENV和CHIKV IgG。研究涉及从八个卫生中心收集样本,并对不同性别、年龄和教育水平群体进行血清流行率分析,同时进行统计学比较。
主要结果
总体ELISA定义的DENV IgG血清流行率为14.3%(95% CI 11.7–17.3%),而CHIKV为0.9%(95% CI 0.4–2.1%)。DENV的最高血清流行率出现在Zibashahr和urban-2区的城区(25%),最低则出现在Kambel Soleiman(4%)。男性DENV血清流行率显著高于女性(17% vs. 10.8%,p = 0.012),且随着教育水平的提高,血清流行率下降(p = 0.021)。年龄较大与DENV血清流行率增加相关(p = 0.040)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,查巴哈尔市总体人群中普遍存在对扎克病毒的先前暴露,且在不同地区和人口统计学特征中显示出显著的血清流行率差异。这突显了了解本地传播动态和风险因素的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了关于扎克病毒在查巴哈尔市的血清流行率的关键见解,可以为高风险地区和人群的针对性监测和控制策略提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于ELISA检测的血清流行率数据,可能无法涵盖所有感染病例。由于研究是横断面调查,且未包括纵向或确认性检测,其证据强度中等。

Seroepidemiology of dengue and chikungunya viruses in Chabahar city, southwestern Iran-2025

Authors: ‪Mostafa Salehi-Vaziri, Tahmineh Jalali, Sana Eybpoosh, Laya Farhan Asadi, Mahsa Tavakoli, Zahra Hosseini, Farnoosh Arbabi, Seyed Marzieh Sajadi, Ehsan Sheykh Noori, Faramarz Mobaraki
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue and chikungunya viruses (DENV, CHIKV), transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, pose significant public health challenges in tropical and sub-tropical regions. This study investigated the seroprevalence of DENV and CHIKV in Chabahar city, an area in which Aedes aegypti has been established and local transmission of DENV reported since 2023. From January to February 2025, a total of 650 serum samples were collected from the catchment area of eight health centers in the city and tested for DENV and CHIKV IgG using ELISA kits. The overall ELISA‐defined DENV IgG seropositivity was 14.3% (95% CI 11.7–17.3%) and for CHIKV was 0.9% (95% CI 0.4–2.1%). The highest prevalence of DENV was observed in the urban areas of Zibashahr and urban-2 Districts (25%), and the lowest in Kambel Soleiman (4%). Men had a significantly higher DENV seroprevalence (17% vs. 10.8% in women, p = 0.012), and seroprevalence decreased with increasing education level ( p = 0.021). Older age was associated with increased DENV seropositivity ( p = 0.040). Occupation was not significantly associated with DENV seropositivity. No significant association was found between mosquito bite reports and seropositivity. These findings provide evidence of ELISA‐defined DENV IgG seropositivity in the general population of Chabahar, suggesting previous exposure to DENV. The high seropositivity in certain districts highlights the need for strengthened surveillance and targeted vector control measures in high risk groups and areas with high Aedes mosquito density.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the seroprevalence of dengue and chikungunya viruses in Chabahar city, southwestern Iran, to address the public health challenges posed by these viruses in tropical and sub-tropical regions. The study was needed due to the reported local transmission of dengue and the potential for chikungunya to spread in the same environment.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Chabahar city, southwestern Iran, from January to February 2025, involving 650 serum samples collected from the catchment area of eight health centers. The population included individuals from different districts and demographics, with a focus on the general population.
Core methods
Serum samples were tested for DENV and CHIKV IgG using ELISA kits. The study involved collecting samples from eight health centers and analyzing them for seroprevalence, with statistical comparisons made between gender, age and education level groups.
Main results
The overall ELISA-defined DENV IgG seropositivity was 14.3% (95% CI 11.7–17.3%) and for CHIKV was 0.9% (95% CI 0.4–2.1%). The highest prevalence of DENV was observed in the urban areas of Zibashahr and urban-2 Districts (25%), and the lowest in Kambel Soleiman (4%). Men had a significantly higher DENV seroprevalence (17% vs. 10.8% in women, p = 0.012), and seroprevalence decreased with increasing education level (p = 0.021). Older age was associated with increased DENV seropositivity (p = 0.040).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that previous exposure to dengue virus is common in the general population of Chabahar, with notable variations in seroprevalence across districts and demographics. This highlights the importance of understanding local transmission dynamics and risk factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical insights into the epidemiology of dengue virus in Chabahar, which can inform targeted surveillance and vector control strategies in high-risk areas and populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to seroprevalence data based on ELISA testing, which may not capture all cases of infection. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on a cross-sectional survey and does not include longitudinal or confirmatory testing.

通过整合免疫信息学和计算方法设计下一代基孔肯雅病毒 疫苗

Designing a next-generation Chikungunya virus vaccine through integrated immunoinformatics and computational approaches
作者： Chandni Hayat, Nayab Gul, Maria D’Elia, Khalid Salmeen Almaary, Haleema Bibi, Sidra Zakir, Muhammad Sadiq, Mulugeta Tesemma, Esmael M. Alyami, Hafiz Muhammad Zohaib
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)是一种通过蚊子传播的α病毒,可引起发热、严重关节痛和神经系统并发症。尽管某些地区已有针对CHIKV的疫苗,但仍需要额外的疫苗策略。本研究采用计算免疫信息学方法,从CHIKV结构多聚蛋白中设计一种多表位疫苗候选物。识别并筛选了B细胞表位和MHC-I及MHC-II表位,并评估了其抗原性、免疫原性、致敏性和毒性。所选表位通过合适的连接子连接,并添加了50 S核糖体L7/L12蛋白作为佐剂。最终构建的候选物显示出良好的预测理化和结构特性,抗原性评分为0.524,并被预测为无致敏性和无毒性。分子对接显示其与TLR-9有良好的相互作用,200 ns的分子动力学模拟表明所建疫苗-受体复合物的稳定性。免疫模拟预测了体液和细胞免疫应答。总体而言,本研究提供了一种计算设计的CHIKV多表位疫苗候选物,可进一步考虑实验评估。需要体外和体内研究来确认其安全性、免疫原性和保护效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过计算免疫信息学方法设计下一代基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)疫苗,以应对额外疫苗策略的需求。
设计与对象
本研究采用计算方法从CHIKV结构多聚蛋白中识别并筛选B细胞和MHC-I/MHC-II表位,评估其抗原性、免疫原性、致敏性和毒性。
核心方法
识别并筛选了B细胞表位和MHC-I/MHC-II表位,评估其抗原性、免疫原性、致敏性和毒性。所选表位通过连接子连接,并添加了50 S核糖体L7/L12蛋白作为佐剂。
主要结果
最终的疫苗构建物显示出良好的预测理化和结构特性,抗原性评分为0.524,并被预测为无致敏性和无毒性。分子对接和分子动力学模拟表明所建疫苗-受体复合物的稳定性,免疫模拟预测了体液和细胞免疫应答。
解释与创新
本研究提供了一种计算设计的CHIKV多表位疫苗候选物,可进一步考虑实验评估,以应对额外疫苗策略的需求。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发一种可评估其安全性、免疫原性和保护效果的CHIKV 疫苗提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖计算预测,需要体外和体内研究来确认疫苗候选物的安全性、免疫原性和保护效果。

Designing a next-generation Chikungunya virus vaccine through integrated immunoinformatics and computational approaches

Authors: Chandni Hayat, Nayab Gul, Maria D’Elia, Khalid Salmeen Almaary, Haleema Bibi, Sidra Zakir, Muhammad Sadiq, Mulugeta Tesemma, Esmael M. Alyami, Hafiz Muhammad Zohaib
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a mosquito-borne alphavirus that can cause fever, severe joint pain and neurological complications. Although vaccines against CHIKV are available in some regions there is still a need for additional vaccine strategies. In this study we used a computational immunoinformatic approach to design a multi-epitope vaccine candidate from the CHIKV structural polyprotein. B-cell epitopes and MHC-I and MHC-II epitopes were identified and screened for antigenicity, immunogenicity, allergenicity and toxicity. The selected epitopes were joined using suitable linkers and a 50 S ribosomal L7/L12 protein was added as an adjuvant. The final construct showed favourable predicted physicochemical and structural properties with an antigenicity score of 0.524. It was also predicted to be non-allergenic and non-toxic. Molecular docking showed favourable interaction with TLR-9, and a 200 ns molecular dynamics simulation indicated stability of the modelled vaccine–receptor complex. Immune simulation predicted both humoral and cellular immune responses. Overall, this study provides a computationally designed CHIKV multi-epitope vaccine candidate that can be considered for further experimental evaluation. In vitro and in vivo studies are needed to confirm its safety, immunogenicity and protective efficacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to design a next-generation Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) vaccine through computational immunoinformatics to address the need for additional vaccine strategies.
Design and population
The study used a computational approach to identify and screen B-cell and MHC-I/MHC-II epitopes from the CHIKV structural polyprotein for antigenicity, immunogenicity, allergenicity, and toxicity.
Core methods
B-cell epitopes and MHC-I/MHC-II epitopes were identified and screened for antigenicity, immunogenicity, allergenicity, and toxicity. The selected epitopes were joined using linkers and a 50 S ribosomal L7/L12 protein was added as an adjuvant.
Main results
The final vaccine construct showed favourable predicted physicochemical and structural properties with an antigenicity score of 0.524, and was predicted to be non-allergenic and non-toxic. Molecular docking and molecular dynamics simulation indicated stability of the modelled vaccine–receptor complex, and immune simulation predicted both humoral and cellular immune responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provided a computationally designed CHIKV multi-epitope vaccine candidate that can be considered for further experimental evaluation, addressing the need for additional vaccine strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of a CHIKV vaccine that can be evaluated for safety, immunogenicity, and protective efficacy in vitro and in vivo.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on computational predictions and the need for in vitro and in vivo studies to confirm the safety, immunogenicity, and protective efficacy of the vaccine candidate.

COVID-19 y chikungunya: desempeño de la vigilancia del dengue en la Triple Frontera Argentina–Brasil–Paraguay

COVID-19 y chikungunya: desempeño de la vigilancia del dengue en la Triple Frontera Argentina–Brasil–Paraguay
作者： Esteban Manuel Couto, Félix Ramírez, Zoraida del Carmen Fernandez Grillo, Leonardo Soares Bastos
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较三边边境地区不同流行病学时期以及疫情与非疫情月份的登革热监测指标,突出该地区在病毒监测和控制中的重要性。
设计与对象
A retrospective observational study was conducted in the Triple Border region, which includes Argentina, Brazil, and Paraguay, focusing on suspected dengue cases reported during four distinct periods: PRE, COVID-19, INTER, and CHIKV.
核心方法
本研究分析了登革热监测的及时性、完整性、一致性以及阳性预测值(PPV),并使用检测阳性率作为补充指标,以评估不同流行病学背景下的监测表现。
主要结果
三边边境地区共报告了228,581例疑似登革热病例,其中160,320例发生在巴西的福斯杜伊瓜苏。在基孔肯雅热紧急时期,福斯杜伊瓜苏的就诊和报告时间最短(平均:2天),而在疫情期间,巴拉圭的埃尔埃斯特的就诊和报告时间分别为1.8天和2天。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,三边边境地区的登革热监测在不同市镇和国家之间表现出异质性,这取决于所评估的流行病学背景。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了在病毒监测中加强整合分析和跨国协调的必要性,以提高该地区的应对效果。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于回顾性数据,这可能引入偏倚并限制建立因果关系的能力。由于研究的观察性质,其证据强度中等。

COVID-19 y chikungunya: desempeño de la vigilancia del dengue en la Triple Frontera Argentina–Brasil–Paraguay

Authors: Esteban Manuel Couto, Félix Ramírez, Zoraida del Carmen Fernandez Grillo, Leonardo Soares Bastos
Original abstract (English)
The Triple Border between Argentina, Brazil, and Paraguay is the largest transboundary urban agglomeration in South America and a critical setting for dengue surveillance and control.Objective. To compare selected dengue surveillance indicators across epidemiological periods and between epidemic and non-epidemic months in the Triple Border.Materials and methods. We conducted a retrospective observational study. We analyzed timeliness (time to consultation or notification and sample collection), completeness, consistency, and positive predictive value (PPV), with test positivity as a complementary indicator. Four periods (PRE, COVID-19, INTER, and CHIKV) and epidemic and non-epidemic dengue months were compared.Results. A total of 228,581 suspected dengue cases were reported in the Triple Frontier, of which 160,320 occurred in Foz do Iguaçu (Brazil). The shortest time to healthcare-seeking and diagnosis was observed in Foz do Iguaçu during the chikungunya emergency (mean: 2 days for both) and in Ciudad del Este (Paraguay) during the pandemic (1.8 and 2 days, respectively). The lowest completeness was observed in Puerto Iguazú (Argentina) in the pre-pandemic period (28.6%) and in Foz do Iguaçu during the chikungunya period (46.4%). Differences between epidemic and non-epidemic months varied by municipality and indicator.Conclusion. Dengue surveillance in the Triple Border showed heterogeneous performance across municipalities and countries across the epidemiological contexts evaluated. These findings highlight the need to strengthen integrated analysis and cross-border coordination of arbovirus surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare dengue surveillance indicators across different epidemiological periods and between epidemic and non-epidemic months in the Triple Border region, highlighting the importance of this area for arbovirus surveillance and control.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted in the Triple Border region, which includes Argentina, Brazil, and Paraguay, focusing on suspected dengue cases reported during four distinct periods: PRE, COVID-19, INTER, and CHIKV.
Core methods
The study analyzed timeliness, completeness, consistency, and positive predictive value (PPV) of dengue surveillance, using test positivity as a complementary indicator to assess performance across different epidemiological contexts.
Main results
A total of 228,581 suspected dengue cases were reported in the Triple Border, with 160,320 occurring in Foz do Iguaçu (Brazil). The shortest time to healthcare-seeking and diagnosis was observed in Foz do Iguaçu during the chikungunya emergency (mean: 2 days) and in Ciudad del Este (Paraguay) during the pandemic (1.8 and 2 days, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that dengue surveillance in the Triple Border showed heterogeneous performance across municipalities and countries, depending on the epidemiological context.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results emphasize the need for improved integrated analysis and cross-border coordination in arbovirus surveillance to enhance response effectiveness in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce biases and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

Question and background
The study aimed to correct errors in the original publication of a chapter on assessing Chikungunya Virus (CHIKV) protease activity and pharmacological inhibition using the acc-CHIK15-dnp fluorogenic peptide substrate. The background highlights the importance of accurate methodologies for studying viral proteases, which are critical for viral replication and antiviral drug development.
Design and population
This is a correction to a book chapter published in Methods in Molecular Biology, which was inadvertently released with errors in text, figure legends, and formatting. The study focuses on the Chikungunya Virus and its protease activity, with the corrected version ensuring accurate representation of the methods and findings.
Core methods
The methods section outlines the use of the acc-CHIK15-dnp fluorogenic peptide substrate to assess protease activity and pharmacological inhibition of Chikungunya Virus. The original version of the chapter had errors in text, figure legends, and formatting, which have been corrected in the updated version.
Main results
The main results of the study include the correction of errors in the original chapter, ensuring that the methods, figure legends, and formatting are accurate and reliable for future research on Chikungunya Virus protease activity and inhibition.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a necessary correction to ensure the accuracy and reliability of the methods described for studying CHIKV protease activity. The novelty lies in the updated and error-free presentation of the protocol.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have significant implications for the accurate study of Chikungunya Virus protease activity, which is essential for developing effective antiviral therapies and understanding viral mechanisms of replication and pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to correcting errors in the original publication, and the evidence strength is high as the corrections ensure the reliability of the methods and data for future research on CHIKV protease activity.

中国境内最大的基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)暴发期间CHIKV基因组的特征 - 对CHIKV干预的启示。

Genomic epidemiology of CHIKV during the largest outbreak in Mainland China - implication for CHIKV intervention.
作者： Li-Na Yi, Si-Ling Xiang, Xu-He Huang, Jian-Hua Huang, Ming-Ji Chen, Heshi Long, Yu-Wei He, Chu-Ting Zeng, Guang-Hu Zhu, Shuyan Tan
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)在全球热带和亚热带地区引发了多次疫情。2025年,中国广东省经历了境内最大的CHIKV暴发,涉及所有21个地级市,共报告了23,464例病例。我们进行了整合的流行病学、基因组和系统发育动力学分析,以研究该暴发的起源、传播动力学以及CHIKV谱系的特征。广东省的病毒株属于东/中/南非(ECSA)基因型、中非谱系(ECSA-MAL)——这是2025年全球共存的几种CHIKV谱系之一;一个长的内部分支将ECSA-MAL与之前测序的近亲分离,表明在流行地区存在显著的传播间隔。使用局部分子钟模型进行的系统发育动力学分析估计,病毒引入发生在2025年4月初的一个狭窄窗口(95% HPD:3月26日-4月12日),表明在检测前存在约2.5个月的隐匿传播期。延长的沉默期与传播动态一致:佛山市北滘镇的蚊虫卵Trap指数在4月保持较低水平(3.0),随后在7月激增至9.0——这与早期传播受到抑制、随后季节性放大相吻合。人类从两个疫情中心的迁徙与病例的空间分布有中等关联(Spearman ρ约为0.5),而蚊虫生态可能限制了空间传播。对全球流行株进行的Phylowave分析识别出33个谱系定义的突变位点,分布在14个亚谱系中,主要集中在NSP3、E1和E2区域。其中四个突变位点已被实验证实为适应性突变。本研究展示了在疫情高峰前加强监测的必要性,并强调了在多样化生态区域监测适应性突变的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)在全球热带和亚热带地区引发了多次疫情。本研究旨在探讨中国境内最大的CHIKV暴发期间CHIKV基因组的特征,以了解其起源和传播动力学。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于2025年中国广东省的CHIKV暴发,该暴发影响了所有21个地级市,共报告了23,464例病例。研究包括对暴发期间整合的流行病学、基因组和系统发育动力学分析。
核心方法
进行了整合的流行病学、基因组和系统发育动力学分析,使用局部分子钟模型来估计病毒引入的时间和传播动力学。同时,还使用Phylowave分析来识别全球流行株中的谱系定义突变位点。
主要结果
广东省的病毒株被鉴定为属于东/中/南非(ECSA)基因型、中非谱系(ECSA-MAL)。一个长的内部分支将ECSA-MAL与之前测序的近亲分离,表明在流行地区存在显著的传播间隔。系统发育动力学分析估计病毒引入发生在2025年4月初的一个狭窄窗口(95% HPD:3月26日-4月12日),表明在检测前存在约2.5个月的隐匿传播期。
解释与创新
延长的沉默期与传播动态一致,因为佛山市北滘镇的蚊虫卵Trap指数在4月保持较低水平(3.0),随后在7月激增至9.0。本研究强调了在疫情高峰前加强监测的重要性,并突出了在多样化生态区域监测适应性突变的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在疫情高峰前加强监测的必要性,并突出了在多样化生态区域监测适应性突变的重要性,以改进干预策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要数据,由于数据范围有限以及流行地区可能存在传播间隔,研究结果的可信度中等。

Genomic epidemiology of CHIKV during the largest outbreak in Mainland China - implication for CHIKV intervention.

Authors: Li-Na Yi, Si-Ling Xiang, Xu-He Huang, Jian-Hua Huang, Ming-Ji Chen, Heshi Long, Yu-Wei He, Chu-Ting Zeng, Guang-Hu Zhu, Shuyan Tan
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) has caused recurrent epidemics across tropical and subtropical regions globally. In 2025, Guangdong, China, experienced the largest documented CHIKV outbreak in mainland China, with 23,464 reported cases across all 21 prefecture-level cities. We conducted integrated epidemiological, genomic, and phylodynamic analyses to investigate the outbreak's origins, transmission dynamics, and CHIKV lineage evolution. The Guangdong strain belongs to the East/Central/South African (ECSA) genotype, Middle African lineage (ECSA-MAL) - one of several CHIKV lineages co-circulating globally in 2025; a long internal branch separates ECSA-MAL from previously sequenced relatives, indicating substantial surveillance gaps in endemic regions. Phylodynamic analysis using a local molecular clock model estimated that the viral introduction occurred within a narrow window in early April 2025 (95% HPD: March 26-April 12), suggesting a 2.5-month period of cryptic transmission prior to detection. The prolonged silent phase aligns with vector dynamics: the Mosquito Ovitrap Index in the outbreak town of Beijiao, Foshan, stayed low in April (3.0) before surging to 9.0 through July - consistent with suppressed early transmission followed by seasonal amplification. Human migration from the two epidemic epicenters was moderately associated with the spatial distribution of cases (Spearman ρ around 0.5), while mosquito ecology may constrain spatial spread. Phylowave analysis on global circulating strains identified 33 lineage-defining mutations across 14 sublineages, concentrated in NSP3, E1 and E2. Four of these have been experimentally validated as adaptive mutations. This study demonstrates the critical need for enhanced genomic surveillance during pre-peak months and underscores the importance of monitoring adaptive mutations across diverse ecological regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) has caused recurrent epidemics across tropical and subtropical regions globally. This study aimed to investigate the genomic epidemiology of CHIKV during the largest outbreak in Mainland China to understand its origins and transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The study focused on the 2025 CHIKV outbreak in Guangdong, China, which affected all 21 prefecture-level cities and resulted in 23,464 reported cases. The research included integrated epidemiological, genomic, and phylodynamic analyses of the outbreak.
Core methods
Integrated epidemiological, genomic, and phylodynamic analyses were conducted using a local molecular clock model to estimate the timing of viral introduction and transmission dynamics. Phylowave analysis was also used to identify lineage-defining mutations across global circulating strains.
Main results
The Guangdong strain was identified as belonging to the East/Central/South African (ECSA) genotype, Middle African lineage (ECSA-MAL). A long internal branch separates ECSA-MAL from previously sequenced relatives, indicating substantial surveillance gaps in endemic regions. Phylodynamic analysis estimated the viral introduction occurred within a narrow window in early April 2025 (95% HPD: March 26-April 12), suggesting a 2.5-month period of cryptic transmission prior to detection.
Interpretation and novelty
The prolonged silent phase aligns with vector dynamics, as the Mosquito Ovitrap Index in Beijiao, Foshan, stayed low in April before surging to 9.0 through July. This study highlights the importance of genomic surveillance during pre-peak months and the monitoring of adaptive mutations across diverse ecological regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for enhanced genomic surveillance during pre-peak months and underscore the importance of monitoring adaptive mutations across diverse ecological regions to improve intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the confidence in the findings is moderate due to the limited scope of the data and the potential for surveillance gaps in endemic regions.

Journal: Archives of virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-14  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00705-026-06788-x  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 171, 12, 324
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

系统性细胞因子谱分析揭示了虫媒病毒感染的不同的促炎特征

Systemic cytokine profiling reveals distinct pro-inflammatory signatures of arboviruses infections.
作者： Isabela Valim Sarmento, Carlos Henrique Fantecelle, Julia Sthefany Nunes Zordan, Bruna Caetano Pimenta, Suwellen Sardinha Dias de Azevedo, Isabela Ribeiro Rodrigues, Anna Clara Gregório Có, Jaqueline Pegoretti Goulart, Eric Arrivabene Tavares, Edson Oliveira Delatorre
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒在热带地区正成为日益严重的公共卫生问题,其中登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和奥罗普切病毒(OROV)经常共同流行并导致重叠的临床表现。在本研究中,我们通过在163名实验室确诊的急性虫媒病毒感染患者中使用多重免疫分析法量化12种细胞因子和趋化因子,对系统性炎症反应进行了表征:无预警症状的DENV1(n = 28)和DENV2(n = 39)患者、CHIKV(n = 42)和OROV(n = 54)患者。还纳入了31名健康对照组(HC,n = 31)。临床观察中,关节痛是唯一能够可靠区分感染的特征,出现在57.1%的CHIKV患者中,而其他组中则为7.4-30.8%;而发热、头痛和肌痛在所有四个组中均有显著重叠。免疫学上,OROV诱导了一种选择性反应,表现为IL-18和CCL2/MCP-1升高,同时IL-1β、TNF-α、IL-12p70、IL-23和IL-17 A降低;DENV1和DENV2则引发了广泛的促炎和抗病毒药物介质激活,其中DENV1涉及的细胞因子范围比DENV2更广;CHIKV则升高了IFN-α2、IFN-γ、IL-18、IL-6、IL-8、MCP-1和IL-17 A。细胞因子网络分析揭示了病毒特异性的免疫连接模式,其中OROV表现出最协调的细胞因子和趋化因子反应,而CHIKV则表现出最碎片化的反应。所有病毒的循环细胞因子浓度在很大程度上与病毒载量无关,表明在急性虫媒病毒感染期间,系统性炎症主要由宿主免疫激活驱动,而非病毒复制动力学。这些发现为DENV、CHIKV和OROV建立了不同的免疫学特征,可能有助于差异化的诊断,并为开发针对特定病原体的治疗策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒在热带地区正成为日益严重的公共卫生问题,其中登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和奥罗普切病毒(OROV)经常共同流行并导致重叠的临床表现。
设计与对象
还纳入了31名健康对照组(HC,n = 31)。
核心方法
在本研究中,我们通过在163名实验室确诊的急性虫媒病毒感染患者中使用多重免疫分析法量化12种细胞因子和趋化因子,对系统性炎症反应进行了表征:无预警症状的DENV1(n = 28)和DENV2(n = 39)患者、CHIKV(n = 42)和OROV(n = 54)患者。
主要结果
细胞因子网络分析揭示了病毒特异性的免疫连接模式,其中OROV表现出最协调的细胞因子和趋化因子反应,而CHIKV则表现出最碎片化的反应。
解释与创新
临床观察中,关节痛是唯一能够可靠区分感染的特征,出现在57.1%的CHIKV患者中,而其他组中则为7.4-30.8%;而发热、头痛和肌痛在所有四个组中均有显著重叠。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为DENV、CHIKV和OROV建立了不同的免疫学特征,可能有助于差异化的诊断,并为开发针对特定病原体的治疗策略提供信息。

所有病毒的循环细胞因子浓度在很大程度上与病毒载量无关,表明在急性虫媒病毒感染期间,系统性炎症主要由宿主免疫激活驱动,而非病毒复制动力学。

Systemic cytokine profiling reveals distinct pro-inflammatory signatures of arboviruses infections.

Authors: Isabela Valim Sarmento, Carlos Henrique Fantecelle, Julia Sthefany Nunes Zordan, Bruna Caetano Pimenta, Suwellen Sardinha Dias de Azevedo, Isabela Ribeiro Rodrigues, Anna Clara Gregório Có, Jaqueline Pegoretti Goulart, Eric Arrivabene Tavares, Edson Oliveira Delatorre
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses are a growing public health concern in tropical regions, where dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and oropouche virus (OROV) frequently co-circulate and cause overlapping clinical manifestations. In this study, we characterised the systemic inflammatory response by quantifying 12 cytokines and chemokines using a multiplex immunoassay in 163 patients with laboratory-confirmed acute arboviral infection: DENV1 (n = 28) and DENV2 (n = 39) patients without warning signs, CHIKV (n = 42) and OROV (n = 54). Thirty-one healthy controls (HC, n = 31) were also included. Clinically, arthralgia was the only feature that reliably distinguished the infections, occurring in 57.1% of CHIKV patients compared with 7.4-30.8% in the other groups, whereas fever, headache and myalgia overlapped substantially across all four. Immunologically, OROV induced a selective response with elevated IL-18 and CCL2/MCP-1 alongside reduced IL-1β, TNF-α, IL-12p70, IL-23 and IL-17 A; DENV1 and DENV2 elicited broad activation of pro-inflammatory and antiviral mediators, with DENV1 engaging a wider range of cytokines than DENV2; and CHIKV elevated IFN-α2, IFN-γ, IL-18, IL-6, IL-8, MCP-1 and IL-17 A. Cytokine network analysis revealed virus-specific patterns of immune connectivity, with OROV showing the most coordinated cytokines and chemokine response and CHIKV the most fragmented. Circulating cytokine concentrations were largely independent of viral load across all viruses, indicating that systemic inflammation during acute arboviral infection is driven primarily by host immune activation rather than by viral replication kinetics. These findings establish distinct immunological signatures for DENV, CHIKV, and OROV that may support differential diagnosis and inform the development of pathogen-specific therapeutic strategies.

使用监测在哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔区鉴定虫媒病毒和其他病毒特征

Identification of Arboviruses and Other Viral Signatures Using Entomo-Virological Surveillance in the Santa Marta District, Colombia.
作者： Juan P Franco-Salazar, Paula Quintero-Cortés, Erik Perdomo-Balaguera, Andrew S Muñoz-Gamba, Angie Duarte-Pilonieta, Juan F Osorio, Gabriel Parra-Henao, José A Usme-Ciro
摘要中文翻译背景与目的 当前针对虫媒病毒疾病的监测策略包括对临床病例进行血清学和病毒学研究,并对媒介的幼虫和成虫阶段的存在和密度进行表征。我们的目的是在哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔区的城乡地区建立一种虫媒-病毒学监测方法,用于检测黄病毒和甲病毒。方法 使用CO2诱饵的CDC灯、Shannon陷阱和配有吸虫器的昆虫网收集蚊虫和沙蝇标本。所有形态类型的标本均被分离并储存,用于通过经典分类学、分子系统学以及使用通用和特异性RT-PCR方法和DNA测序进行病毒检测。序列通过MAFFT对齐,系统发育树通过IQ-TREE重建。结果 共收集了2953份标本。识别出多种媒介物种,其中Ae. aegypti和Culex sp.在城乡地区均占主导地位。病毒检测和测序结果显示,Orthoflavivirus denguei(DENV)型1存在基因型V(美洲/非洲)的流行;线系1V_D.1和1V_D.2同时流行,且具有不同的近期起源;基于分子检测但未进行序列确认,推测存在Orthoflavivirus zikaense(ZIKV)的隐匿性流行。在研究期间,未在测试的池中检测到甲病毒基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)。此外,在Ae.的基因组中检测到Culex黄病毒和一个可能源自 Menghai 类黄病毒的非逆转录病毒整合RNA病毒(NIRV)元件。解释与结论 本研究展示了DENV在媒介种群中的流行,并提供了ZIKV的推测性分子证据。对黄病毒阳性样本的深入分析使得检测到Culex黄病毒,以及在Ae. aegypti的基因组中发现一个可能的NIRV元件,其对媒介的媒介能力及虫媒病毒动态的影响值得进一步研究。

Identification of Arboviruses and Other Viral Signatures Using Entomo-Virological Surveillance in the Santa Marta District, Colombia.

Authors: Juan P Franco-Salazar, Paula Quintero-Cortés, Erik Perdomo-Balaguera, Andrew S Muñoz-Gamba, Angie Duarte-Pilonieta, Juan F Osorio, Gabriel Parra-Henao, José A Usme-Ciro
Original abstract (English)
Background and objective Current surveillance strategies for arboviral diseases involve serological and virological study of clinical cases and characterization of presence and densities of immature and mature stages of vectors. Our objective was to establish an entomo-virological surveillance of flaviviruses and alphaviruses in urban and rural areas of the Santa Marta district, Colombia. Methods Mosquitoes and sandfly specimens were collected using CO 2 -baited CDC light, Shannon traps, and entomological nets with aspirators. Specimens belonging to every morphotype were separated and stored for processing through classical taxonomy, molecular systematics, and for virus detection using generic, specific RT-PCR methods, and DNA sequencing. Sequences were aligned through MAFFT and phylogenetic trees reconstructed through IQ-TREE. Results A total of 2953 specimens were collected. Several species of vectors were identified, with Ae. aegypti and Culex sp. being predominant in both urban and rural areas. Viral detection and sequencing revealed the circulation of genotype V (American/African) of Orthoflavivirus denguei (DENV) type 1, cocirculation of lineages 1V_D.1 and 1V_D.2 with different recent origins, and presumptive cryptic circulation of Orthoflavivirus zikaense (ZIKV) based on molecular detection without sequence confirmation. Alphavirus chikungunya (CHIKV) was not detected in the tested pools during the study period. In addition, the presence of Culex flavivirus and a putative nonretroviral integrated RNA virus (NIRV) element derived from a Menghai -like flavivirus was detected in the genome of Ae. Interpretation and conclusion This study demonstrated the circulation of DENV in the vector population and provided presumptive molecular evidence of ZIKV. In-depth analysis of flavivirus-positive samples allowed the detection of the Culex flavivirus , as well as a putative NIRV element in the genome of Ae. aegypti , whose effects on vector competence and arboviruses dynamics justify further research.

全球 基因组监测。

Global Genomic Surveillance.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。

Global Genomic Surveillance.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response. This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV). It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability. The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance. By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.

Scientific and public-health significance
This chapter highlights the importance of genomic surveillance in public health, emphasizing the need for sustainable, equitable, and interoperable systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The chapter discusses critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability, which limit the effectiveness of genomic surveillance systems.

编者按:RNA病毒:从分子机制到新型治疗靶向策略

Editorial: RNA viruses: from molecular mechanisms to novel therapeutic targeting strategies
作者： Elena Criscuolo, Alfredo Berzal‐Herranz
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
RNA病毒仍然是公共卫生领域最重要的挑战之一。它们的紧凑基因组、高突变率以及能够利用宿主细胞机制的能力,使它们能够快速适应、跨物种溢出,并逃避自然免疫和治疗压力。
设计与对象
最终的集合非常紧凑:四篇文章涵盖了一个病例报告、一项生物标志物研究、一项转录组研究和一项表观转录组研究。它们还突出了这些方法的机会和局限性。在没有先入为主假设的情况下检测RNA病毒。
核心方法
重新分析公开的全血RNA测序数据集,以确定环状RNA(circRNAs)是否可以作为基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)感染的早期诊断标志物,这是一种重新出现的蚊媒疾病,目前尚无疫苗或抗病毒药物获批,且RT-PCR和血清学可能在疾病初期几天内漏检。利用RNA测序分析糖尿病足溃疡(DFU)患者和对照组的皮肤组织,以研究非编码RNA(lncRNAs)与该疾病常伴随的贫血之间的关系。使用m6A微阵列分析异位与正常子宫内膜中mRNA的修饰情况,鉴定出8,333个异常甲基化的转录本。
主要结果
In a discovery cohort of 39 acute patients and 20 healthy controls, they defined a signature of 13 circRNAs (9 upregulated and 4 downregulated), summarized in a weighted "CHIKV-index." In an independent pediatric cohort of 43 children, the index distinguished acute from convalescent samples (AUC = 0.94) and correlated with viral load, but not with disease severity. They identified 21 differentially expressed lncRNAs (one upregulated and twenty downregulated) and 368 differentially expressed mRNAs, with enrichment of iron uptake and transport, oxidative stress, and neutrophil degranulation pathways.
解释与创新
白等人报告的环状RNA特征,其父基因包括干扰素刺激基因,展示了宿主非编码RNA如何反映病毒感染。因此,这些论文中描述的工作流程可以直接应用于感染细胞和组织的研究,在这些研究中,宿主和病毒RNA会被共同分析。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了一个具体的例子,说明一个宿主非编码RNA特征可能如何补充病原体检测。
局限与证据强度
在发现集中观察到的完美区分属于样本内结果,因此可能过于乐观;验证阶段比较的是同一批儿童的急性期与恢复期样本,而非感染与未感染个体之间的比较;特别是针对临床相似的虫媒病毒,特异性仍需进一步确立。小样本量、缺乏正交验证以及依赖相关性或计算机模拟预测是反复出现的局限性。

Editorial: RNA viruses: from molecular mechanisms to novel therapeutic targeting strategies

Authors: Elena Criscuolo, Alfredo Berzal‐Herranz
Original abstract (English)
RNA viruses remain among the most significant challenges to public health. Their compact genomes, high mutation rates, and capacity to exploit host cellular machinery allow them to adapt rapidly, cross species barriers, and escape both natural immunity and therapeutic pressure. Understanding how these pathogens enter cells, replicate, evolve, and interact with their hosts is therefore a prerequisite for designing better antivirals, vaccines, and diagnostics. This Research Topic was launched in Frontiers in Genetics to bring together work on the comparative genetics and functional dynamics of RNA viruses, host-virus interactions and pathogenicity, mutation and evolution, and strategies targeting different stages of the viral life cycle, including emerging technologies such as CRISPR-based tools. The resulting collection is compact: four articles spanning a case report, a biomarker study, a transcriptomic study, and an epitranscriptomic study. Taken together, they do not map the whole field, but they illustrate three complementary ways in which RNA biology, in both virus and host, is being interrogated using genome-scale methods. They also highlight both the opportunities and the limitations of these approaches.Detecting an RNA virus without prior assumptions. report the case of a 6-month-old girl admitted with severe acute hepatitis of unknown etiology, in whom an extensive panel of virological, metabolic, genetic, and autoimmune investigations was negative. Shotgun metagenomic sequencing of plasma, using sequence-independent single-primer amplification for enrichment, recovered the complete 9,187-bp genome of human pegivirus 1 (HPgV-1; family Flaviviridae) at a mean coverage of approximately 447×; no other pathogen was identified. Phylogenetic analysis placed the sequence on a distinct branch within genotype 1, close to strains reported from Thailand and Denmark, and the authors describe two highly conserved amino acid substitutions in NS3. The child recovered with supportive care alone. The authors are appropriately cautious: detection of a viral genome in plasma does not demonstrate causation, and this is a single case without viral-load data. Nevertheless, the report shows how unbiased sequencing can yield a complete, analyzable genome when targeted diagnostics fail, and it returns a long-debated virus, whose pathogenic potential has been contested for years, to the discussion of pediatric hepatitis of unknown origin.A host circular RNA signature of arboviral infection. re-analyzed public whole-blood RNA-seq datasets to determine whether circular RNAs (circRNAs) can serve as early diagnostic markers of Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) infection, a re-emerging mosquito-borne disease for which no vaccine or antiviral is approved and for which RT-PCR and serology may miss the first days of illness. In a discovery cohort of 39 acute patients and 20 healthy controls, they defined a signature of 13 circRNAs (9 upregulated and 4 downregulated), summarized in a weighted "CHIKV-index." In an independent pediatric cohort of 43 children, the index distinguished acute from convalescent samples (AUC = 0.94) and correlated with viral load, but not with disease severity. Enrichment analysis pointed to myeloid activation and cytokine signaling, and several parental genes are interferon-stimulated genes, including ZNFX1 and PARP12. The perfect discrimination observed in the discovery set is in-sample and therefore likely optimistic; the validation compared acute with convalescent samples from the same children rather than infected with uninfected individuals; and specificity, particularly against clinically similar arboviruses, remains to be established. The work nonetheless provides a concrete example of how a host non-coding RNA signature might complement pathogen detection.The non-coding transcriptome of a chronic wound. used RNA sequencing of skin tissue from patients with diabetic foot ulcers (DFU) and controls to investigate how long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) relate to the anemia that frequently accompanies this condition. They identified 21 differentially expressed lncRNAs (one upregulated and twenty downregulated) and 368 differentially expressed mRNAs, with enrichment of iron uptake and transport, oxidative stress, and neutrophil degranulation pathways. They also built a co-expression network in which LINC02397 emerged as a hub. The study is exploratory: it is based on eight individuals, the controls were patients with acute wounds rather than healthy skin, and the diagnostic and mechanistic implications raised in the discussion, such as restoring or silencing specific lncRNAs, remain hypotheses to be tested. modification of mRNAs in ectopic versus normal endometrium using an m6A microarray, identifying 8,333 aberrantly methylated transcripts. Network analysis prioritized 15 hub genes, and integration with a public dataset, receiver operating characteristic analysis, qRT-PCR on clinical samples, and computational drug-repurposing and docking analyses pointed to VCAM1 and RAP1A as leading candidates, together with several small molecules as possible starting points for further study. As the authors acknowledge, the sample size for the array was small, qRT-PCR measured expression rather than methylation, and the pharmacological evidence is computational only.The four studies address different diseases and, importantly, only two of them deal directly with RNA viruses. We nevertheless consider it valuable to read them side by side for three reasons.First, all four exemplify hypothesis-free, data-driven approaches, whether through the unbiased recovery of a viral genome from plasma or the global profiling of circRNA, lncRNA, and m6A landscapes. Such approaches are well suited to situations in which the relevant players are not known in advance, as in an unexplained clinical syndrome or a poorly characterized regulatory layer.Second, they highlight RNA as both an informational molecule and a regulatory substrate. Non-coding RNAs, including lncRNAs and circRNAs, and RNA modifications such as m6A shape the host transcriptome in inflammatory and tissue-repair contexts, the same contexts in which RNA viruses replicate and cause disease.Epitranscriptomic marks are increasingly recognized as modulators of RNA virus replication and innate immune sensing, and lncRNAs participate in antiviral responses. The circRNA signature reported by Bai et al., whose parental genes include interferon-stimulated genes, illustrates how host non-coding RNAs can reflect viral infection. The workflows described in these papers could therefore be transferred directly to the study of infected cells and tissues, where host and viral RNAs are profiled together. We regard mechanistic work in infected cells and tissues as a natural next step, since none of the present articles test this directly.Third, the articles collectively illustrate the distance between a candidate and a mechanism. In each study, the most attractive conclusions, such as a causal role for HPgV-1 in hepatitis, the generalizability of a CHIKV circRNA signature, lncRNA-based therapeutic modulation, or repurposed compounds targeting VCAM1 and RAP1A, are those that the current data cannot yet support. Small cohorts, lack of orthogonal validation, and reliance on correlation or in silico predictions are recurrent limitations. We encourage future work in this area to pair discovery with functional experiments, such as infection or perturbation models, and with adequately powered, independently validated cohorts.Even with a limited number of contributions, this Research Topic captures current trends in RNA virology and RNA biology more broadly. Sequencing technologies now make it possible to study viral and host RNAs in unprecedented detail, shifting the challenge from generating data to establishing causality and clinical relevance.For the clinician, metagenomic sequencing offers a route to etiological diagnosis when conventional tests are uninformative, while host RNA signatures, such as the circRNA index described for Chikungunya, may in time complement pathogen detection, provided that their specificity and performance are demonstrated in prospective cohorts. For the molecular biologist, lncRNAs and RNA modifications offer additional layers of regulation that may be exploited therapeutically, in both virus-related and non-virus-related diseases. For both communities, rigorous study design, transparent reporting, and functional validation will determine which of today candidates become tomorrow targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
RNA viruses remain among the most significant challenges to public health. Their compact genomes, high mutation rates, and capacity to exploit host cellular machinery allow them to adapt rapidly, cross species barriers, and escape both natural immunity and therapeutic pressure.
Design and population
The resulting collection is compact: four articles spanning a case report, a biomarker study, a transcriptomic study, and an epitranscriptomic study. They also highlight both the opportunities and the limitations of these approaches. Detecting an RNA virus without prior assumptions.
Core methods
re-analyzed public whole-blood RNA-seq datasets to determine whether circular RNAs (circRNAs) can serve as early diagnostic markers of Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) infection, a re-emerging mosquito-borne disease for which no vaccine or antiviral is approved and for which RT-PCR and serology may miss the first days of illness. used RNA sequencing of skin tissue from patients with diabetic foot ulcers (DFU) and controls to investigate how long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) relate to the anemia that frequently accompanies this condition. modification of mRNAs in ectopic versus normal endometrium using an m6A microarray, identifying 8,333 aberrantly methylated transcripts.
Main results
In a discovery cohort of 39 acute patients and 20 healthy controls, they defined a signature of 13 circRNAs (9 upregulated and 4 downregulated), summarized in a weighted "CHIKV-index." In an independent pediatric cohort of 43 children, the index distinguished acute from convalescent samples (AUC = 0.94) and correlated with viral load, but not with disease severity. They identified 21 differentially expressed lncRNAs (one upregulated and twenty downregulated) and 368 differentially expressed mRNAs, with enrichment of iron uptake and transport, oxidative stress, and neutrophil degranulation pathways.
Interpretation and novelty
The circRNA signature reported by Bai et al., whose parental genes include interferon-stimulated genes, illustrates how host non-coding RNAs can reflect viral infection. The workflows described in these papers could therefore be transferred directly to the study of infected cells and tissues, where host and viral RNAs are profiled together.
Scientific and public-health significance
The work provides a concrete example of how a host non-coding RNA signature might complement pathogen detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The perfect discrimination observed in the discovery set is in-sample and therefore likely optimistic; the validation compared acute with convalescent samples from the same children rather than infected with uninfected individuals; and specificity, particularly against clinically similar arboviruses, remains to be established. Small cohorts, lack of orthogonal validation, and reliance on correlation or in silico predictions are recurrent limitations.

分子克隆和反向遗传学。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.
作者： Bardossy ES
摘要中文翻译本章介绍了使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 的详细分子生物学协议。该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。该文件的结构包括清晰的介绍、所有必需试剂和设备的列表以及分步方法部分。

问题与背景
该研究旨在建立一个分子生物学方案,将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组,以更好地了解其遗传特征和 突变 的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究描述了详细的分子生物学方案,使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组,重点关注该病毒的东部/中部/南非和亚洲谱系。
核心方法
该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome to better understand its genetic characteristics and potential for mutation.
Design and population
The study describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome using a reverse genetics strategy, focusing on the East/Central/South African and Asian lineages of the virus.
Core methods
The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep.
Main results
The protocol was successfully implemented to introduce specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome, with all steps completed as described.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a comprehensive and detailed method for introducing point mutations into the CHIKV genome, which is novel in its application of reverse genetics for molecular cloning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable tool for studying the genetic variability of CHIKV, which can inform future research on viral evolution and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not include experimental results or validation of the method's effectiveness. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a detailed description of the procedure rather than empirical findings.

蚊媒病毒感染病程中Galectin-7的差异表达

Differential expression of Galectin-7 in the clinical course of arboviral diseases.
作者： Carlos Alonso Domínguez-Alemán, Francisco Javier Cabrera-Jorge, Karina Guadalupe Hernández-Flores, Andrea Isabel Torres-Zugaide, Ricardo Remes-Ruíz, Peter Grube-Pagola, Leticia Cedillo-Barrón, Héctor Vivanco-Cid
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:我们评估了Galectin-7(Gal-7)的产生及其与临床蚊媒病毒感染参数的相关性,探讨其作为宿主分子在效应反应中的作用及其作为蚊媒病毒感染新生物标志物的潜力。方法:我们检测了确诊登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒感染患者的血清Gal-7水平。我们比较了根据症状天数定义的临床阶段中的血清Gal-7水平及其与血象/生化参数的相关性。我们使用ROC曲线评估Gal-7作为生物标志物,单独或与谷草转氨酶(GOT)联合使用,以区分健康个体与患者,并区分不同的蚊媒病毒。结果:登革热和基孔肯雅热患者的血清Gal-7水平显著高于寨卡热患者或健康对照组。在登革热患者中,Gal-7与皮疹等临床症状以及血象参数(特别是淋巴细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞)呈正相关。这种Gal-7升高在登革热患者发病后五天内即可观察到。虽然Gal-7能够有效区分登革热患者与健康对照组,但其在区分三种蚊媒病毒方面的能力有限。然而,将Gal-7与GOT结合成双标志物诊断面板显著提高了对登革热病毒与其他蚊媒病毒(如寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒)感染的鉴别能力。结论:我们的研究首次显示,登革热和基孔肯雅热患者血清中Gal-7水平显著升高,而寨卡热患者则没有这种现象。当将Gal-7与GOT结合成双标志物面板时,可以提供一种可靠的诊断工具,用于准确区分共循环的蚊媒病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估Galectin-7(Gal-7)的产生及其与临床蚊媒病毒感染参数的相关性,探讨其作为宿主分子在效应反应中的作用及其作为蚊媒病毒感染新生物标志物的潜力。
设计与对象
本研究比较了确诊登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒感染患者的血清Gal-7水平,分析其在根据症状天数定义的临床阶段中与血象和生化参数的相关性。
核心方法
检测了确诊登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒感染患者的血清Gal-7水平。比较了不同症状天数定义的临床阶段中的血清Gal-7水平,并分析其与血象/生化参数的相关性。使用ROC曲线评估Gal-7作为生物标志物,单独或与谷草转氨酶(GOT)联合使用,以区分健康个体与患者,并区分不同的蚊媒病毒。
主要结果
登革热和基孔肯雅热患者的血清Gal-7水平显著高于寨卡热患者或健康对照组。在登革热患者中,Gal-7与皮疹等临床症状以及血象参数(特别是淋巴细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞)呈正相关。这种升高在登革热患者发病后五天内即可观察到。虽然Gal-7能够有效区分登革热患者与健康对照组,但其在区分三种蚊媒病毒方面的能力有限。然而,将Gal-7与GOT结合成双标志物诊断面板显著提高了对登革热病毒与其他蚊媒病毒(如寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒)感染的鉴别能力。
解释与创新
作者认为,他们的研究结果首次表明,登革热和基孔肯雅热患者的血清Gal-7水平显著升高,而寨卡热患者则没有这种现象。他们得出结论,将Gal-7与GOT结合成双标志物面板可以提供一种可靠的诊断工具,用于准确区分共循环的蚊媒病毒。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Gal-7可能成为蚊媒病毒感染差异诊断的有价值的生物标志物,特别是在区分登革热和基孔肯雅热与寨卡热方面。这可能有助于改善这些病毒共循环地区的诊断策略和监测工作。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于仅关注血清Gal-7水平,并且与其他病毒相比仅使用单一生物标志物(Gal-7)。研究证据基于摘要数据,由于研究设计和特定关注Gal-7作为生物标志物,其结果的支持程度中等。

Differential expression of Galectin-7 in the clinical course of arboviral diseases.

Authors: Carlos Alonso Domínguez-Alemán, Francisco Javier Cabrera-Jorge, Karina Guadalupe Hernández-Flores, Andrea Isabel Torres-Zugaide, Ricardo Remes-Ruíz, Peter Grube-Pagola, Leticia Cedillo-Barrón, Héctor Vivanco-Cid
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: We evaluated Galectin-7 (Gal-7) production and its correlation with clinical arboviral disease parameters, exploring both, its role as a host molecule acting during the effector response and its potential as a new biomarker in arboviral diseases. METHODS: We determined serum Gal-7 levels in patients with confirmed dengue, chikungunya, and Zika infection. We compared serum Gal-7 levels across symptom-day-defined clinical phases and their correlation with hematological/biochemical parameters. We used ROC curves to evaluate Gal-7, both individually and alongside Glutamic Oxaloacetic Transaminase (GOT), as biomarkers to distinguish healthy individuals from patients and to discriminate between arboviruses. RESULTS: Serum Gal-7 levels were significantly higher in patients with dengue and chikungunya compared to those with Zika or healthy controls. In dengue patients, Gal-7 positively correlated with clinical signs like exanthema and hematological parameters, specifically lymphocytes and basophils. This Gal-7 elevation was evident within the first five days of symptom onset in dengue patients. While Gal-7 effectively discriminated dengue patients from healthy controls, it had limited ability to differentiate among the three arboviruses. However, combining Gal-7 with GOT into a dual-marker diagnostic panel significantly enhanced the differentiation of dengue virus infections from both Zika and Chikungunya. CONCLUSIONS: Our results demonstrate, for the first time, significant elevations in Gal-7 serum levels in dengue and chikungunya but not Zika patients. When integrated into a dual-marker panel with GOT, it offers a robust diagnostic tool for the accurate differential discrimination of co-circulating arboviruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate Galectin-7 (Gal-7) production and its correlation with clinical arboviral disease parameters, exploring its role as a host molecule during the effector response and its potential as a new biomarker in arboviral diseases.
Design and population
The study compared serum Gal-7 levels in patients with confirmed dengue, chikungunya, and Zika infections, analyzing their correlation with hematological and biochemical parameters across symptom-day-defined clinical phases.
Core methods
Serum Gal-7 levels were determined in patients with confirmed dengue, chikungunya, and Zika infections. The levels were compared across symptom-day-defined clinical phases and correlated with hematological/biochemical parameters. ROC curves were used to assess Gal-7, both individually and alongside GOT, as biomarkers to distinguish healthy individuals from patients and to differentiate between arboviruses.
Main results
Serum Gal-7 levels were significantly higher in patients with dengue and chikungunya compared to those with Zika or healthy controls. In dengue patients, Gal-7 positively correlated with clinical signs like exanthema and hematological parameters, specifically lymphocytes and basophils. This elevation was evident within the first five days of symptom onset in dengue patients. While Gal-7 effectively discriminated dengue patients from healthy controls, it had limited ability to differentiate among the three arboviruses. However, combining Gal-7 with GOT into a dual-marker diagnostic panel significantly enhanced the differentiation of dengue virus infections from both Zika and Chikungunya.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that their findings demonstrate, for the first time, significant elevations in Gal-7 serum levels in dengue and chikungunya but not Zika patients. They concluded that integrating Gal-7 with GOT into a dual-marker panel offers a robust diagnostic tool for accurately differentiating co-circulating arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Gal-7 could be a valuable biomarker for the differential diagnosis of arboviral diseases, particularly in distinguishing dengue and chikungunya from Zika. This could inform improved diagnostic strategies and surveillance efforts in regions where these viruses co-circulate.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on serum Gal-7 levels and the use of a single biomarker (Gal-7) in comparison to other viruses. The evidence is based on abstract-only data, and the results are supported by a moderate level of confidence due to the study's design and the specific focus on Gal-7 as a biomarker.

巴西北部无症状献血者中虫媒病毒的分子筛查。

Molecular screening of arboviruses in asymptomatic blood donors from Northern Brazil.
作者： Alexandre Rodrigues Calazans, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Marisa de Oliveira Ribeiro, Barbara Araújo Nogueira, Élida Millena de Vasconcelos Brandão, Katia Cristina Silva Nascimento, Luiz Paulo de Brito Oliveira Souza, Priscilla Lopes da Silva Guimarães, Pedro Henrique Cardoso, Alexandre Vicente Frederico
摘要中文翻译背景:由登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)引起的无症状感染对血液安全构成潜在风险,因为病毒血症献血者可能传播具有严重后果的输血传播感染。方法:使用ChemagicTM PrimeTM平台,从巴西NAT PLUS系统中收集的亚马逊州血液病学与输血病学医院基金会(HEMOAM)合并血浆样本中提取了5,954个池(每个池包含六个样本)的病毒RNA,并通过RT-qPCR使用ZC D-Tipagem分子检测方法(Bio-Manguinhos)进行分析。结果:在连续十八个流行病学周内,共检测到11例CHIKV阳性病例、4例DENV阳性病例,而在血袋样本中未检测到ZIKV阳性病例。结论:在雨季期间检测到由虫媒病毒传播引起的无症状感染,表明该地区存在持续的隐性病毒循环,并强调了对献血者监测进行监测的重要性。

Molecular screening of arboviruses in asymptomatic blood donors from Northern Brazil.

Authors: Alexandre Rodrigues Calazans, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Marisa de Oliveira Ribeiro, Barbara Araújo Nogueira, Élida Millena de Vasconcelos Brandão, Katia Cristina Silva Nascimento, Luiz Paulo de Brito Oliveira Souza, Priscilla Lopes da Silva Guimarães, Pedro Henrique Cardoso, Alexandre Vicente Frederico
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Asymptomatic infections caused by the arboviruses dengue virus (DENV), zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) pose a hidden risk to blood safety, as viremic donors may contribute to transfusion-transmitted infections with severe outcomes. METHODS: Viral RNA from 5,954 pools (each pool contains six samples) was extracted using the ChemagicTM PrimeTM platform, as part of the Brazilian NAT PLUS system from pooled plasma samples collected at Amazonas Hematology and Hemotherapy Hospital Foundation (HEMOAM) and analyzed by RT-qPCR using the ZC D-Tipagem molecular assay (Bio-Manguinhos). RESULTS: Over eighteen consecutive epidemiological weeks, 11 CHIKV-positive cases were identified, 4 DENV-positive cases, and no ZIKV-positive detections on the blood bags samples analyzed. CONCLUSION: The detection of asymptomatic arboviral infections during the rainy season, when environmental conditions favor mosquito proliferation and arbovirus transmission, suggests ongoing silent viral circulation in the region and reinforces the importance of blood donor surveillance.

Question and background
Asymptomatic infections caused by the arboviruses dengue virus (DENV), zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) pose a hidden risk to blood safety, as viremic donors may contribute to transfusion-transmitted infections with severe outcomes.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

使用报告病毒筛选针对 CHIKV 的 抗病毒药物 制剂。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.
作者： Castro EF, Alvarez D
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。因此,迫切需要鉴定和开发新的抗 CHIKV 活性分子。在这里,我们提出了一种基于细胞的方法,能够实施快速且经济高效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。该方法允许识别针对 CHIKV 具有良好活性和选择性的新型 抗病毒药物 命中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。
设计与对象
该研究旨在识别和开发针对 CHIKV 的新活性分子。该研究的重点是开发基于细胞的化合物高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)方法。
核心方法
该研究采用基于细胞的方法来实施快速且经济有效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。
主要结果
该方法能够识别新的 抗病毒药物 命中,对 CHIKV 具有良好的活性和选择性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了他们的方法论识别有效的 抗病毒药物 药物对抗 CHIKV 的潜力。这种方法代表了一种筛选抗 CHIKV 化合物的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 CHIKV 新 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,这可以帮助更有效地控制疾病并减轻慢性肌肉骨骼炎症的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于提出一种方法及其识别 抗病毒药物 药物的潜力,没有报告具体结果或验证。证据是温和的,因为它是基于提议的策略而不是确认的结果。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Authors: Castro EF, Alvarez D
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms. Thus, the identification and development of new active molecules against CHIKV is urgently needed. Here, we present a cell-based methodology that enables the implementation of a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules. This methodology allows for the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms.
Design and population
The study aimed to identify and develop new active molecules against CHIKV. The research focused on the development of a cell-based methodology for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds.
Core methods
The study employed a cell-based methodology to implement a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules.
Main results
The methodology enabled the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the potential of their methodology to identify effective antiviral agents against CHIKV. This approach represents a novel strategy for screening compounds against CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of new antiviral therapies for CHIKV, which could help manage the disease more effectively and reduce the burden of chronic musculoskeletal inflammation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to presenting a methodology and its potential for identifying antiviral agents, without reporting specific results or validation. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a proposed strategy rather than confirmed outcomes.

表位标记和免疫共沉淀鉴定病毒蛋白相互作用物。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.
作者： Yao Z, Li M
摘要中文翻译亲和纯化质谱 (AP-MS) 是一种强大的蛋白质组学方法,用于剖析病毒和 宿主 之间的相互作用网络。传统的 AP-MS 采用病毒蛋白的过度表达作为诱饵来丰富 宿主 相互作用子。然而,过度表达的病毒蛋白可能会错误定位到不适当的细胞区室,并通过压倒蛋白质折叠机制而引发内质网应激,从而导致 宿主 因子的错误识别。为了克服这些限制,我们引入了一种基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,我们用它成功地发现了 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主 巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。在本协议中,我们将逐步描述该技术:(1)通过先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记病毒,(2)体外转录和制备感染性 myc 标记病毒库存,(3)免疫沉淀 myc 标记病毒蛋白及其相互作用组进行质谱分析。该策略能够在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用因子,为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种更准确的策略来识别生理相关背景下的病毒蛋白相互作用物,从而解决传统 AP-MS 方法的局限性。这一背景凸显了了解病毒-宿主 相互作用对于 抗病毒药物 研究的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,以发现 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主(特别是巨噬细胞)中的新 抗病毒药物 因子。
核心方法
该方法包括使用先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记的 基孔肯雅病毒,通过体外转录制备感染性 myc 标记的病毒原液,并对 myc 标记的病毒蛋白进行免疫沉淀,然后对其相互作用组进行质谱分析。
主要结果
该研究使用所描述的 AP-MS 策略成功地鉴定了 CHIKV 感染的巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的新型 AP-MS 策略在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用物的有效性,这为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究病毒-宿主 相互作用提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来对 抗病毒药物 因子的研究提供信息,并可能有助于 基孔肯雅病毒 感染靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对该方法及其应用的抽象描述,由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.

Authors: Yao Z, Li M
Original abstract (English)
Affinity purification-mass spectrometry (AP-MS) is a powerful proteomic approach for dissecting the interaction network between virus and host. Traditional AP-MS employs overexpression of viral proteins as baits to enrich host interactors. However, overexpressed viral proteins may mislocalize to inappropriate cellular compartments and trigger endoplasmic reticulum stress by overwhelming the protein-folding machinery, which leads to false identification of host factors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2), which we used to successfully uncover two new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs-macrophages. In this protocol, we will describe this technique step by step: (1) design and construction of myc-tagged virus by advanced multi-fragment assembly, (2) in vitro transcription and preparation of infectious myc-tagged virus stocks, and (3) immunoprecipitation of myc-tagged viral protein and its interactome for mass spectrometry analysis. This strategy enables accurate identification of viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, providing a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional AP-MS methods by developing a more accurate strategy for identifying viral protein interactors in a physiologically relevant context. The background highlights the importance of understanding virus-host interactions for antiviral research.
Design and population
The study introduced an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2) to uncover new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs, specifically macrophages.
Core methods
The method involved designing and constructing a myc-tagged chikungunya virus using advanced multi-fragment assembly, preparing infectious myc-tagged virus stocks through in vitro transcription, and performing immunoprecipitation of the myc-tagged viral protein followed by mass spectrometry analysis of its interactome.
Main results
The study successfully identified two new antiviral factors in CHIKV-infected macrophages using the described AP-MS strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of their novel AP-MS strategy in accurately identifying viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, which provides a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying virus-host interactions, which can inform future research on antiviral factors and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only description of the method and its application, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analysis.

质粒分子工程的无限制克隆方法。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.
作者： Cerf NT, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。使用持续的高保真聚合酶可显着减少碱基替换错误的发生,确保整个扩增过程中的序列完整性。传统上,限制性内切酶被用来促进定向克隆。然而,替代方法允许诱变,而不需要独特和特定的限制位点,并且可以应用于几乎任何克隆或无缝 DNA 组装策略。在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。该方法能够对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、高效和可靠的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种不使用限制性酶的质粒分子工程方法,满足对更灵活、更有效的基因操作克隆技术的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种适用于任何环状 媒介 的无限制和无连接 PCR 方案,重点关注生成 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 突变体构建体的分子克隆过程。
核心方法
使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆,以实现克隆 媒介 中特定 DNA 片段的插入、替换或修饰,通过使用持续性、高保真度聚合酶确保序列完整性。
主要结果
该方法允许对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、有效和可靠的策略。
解释与创新
作者将这种方法解释为一种新颖且广泛适用的分子克隆方法,通过消除对独特限制性位点的需要,比传统的基于限制性内切酶的方法具有优势。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 的病毒学和分子研究提供信息,支持对其遗传结构、功能和潜在治疗或诊断应用的研究。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告局限性或证据强度,但该方法被描述为稳健且通用的,并且基于其详细描述和对 CHIKV 的应用进行了高置信度评估。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.

Authors: Cerf NT, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences. This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses. The use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases significantly reduces the occurrence of base substitution errors, ensuring sequence integrity throughout the amplification process. Traditionally, restriction enzymes have been employed to facilitate directional cloning; however, alternative methods allow for mutagenesis without the need for unique and specific restriction sites and can be applied to virtually any cloning or seamless DNA assembly strategy. In this chapter, we describe a restriction enzyme-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol widely applicable to any circular vector. This method enables targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, offering a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for molecular engineering of plasmids without the use of restriction enzymes, addressing the need for a more flexible and efficient cloning technique for genetic manipulation.
Design and population
The study describes a restriction-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol applicable to any circular vector, focusing on the molecular cloning process for generating mutant constructs of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome.
Core methods
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase was employed to enable the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, ensuring sequence integrity through the use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases.
Main results
The method allows for targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, providing a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this method as a novel and widely applicable approach for molecular cloning, offering advantages over traditional restriction enzyme-based methods by eliminating the need for unique restriction sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method can inform virological and molecular studies of chikungunya virus, supporting research into its genetic structure, function, and potential therapeutic or diagnostic applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength, but the method is described as robust and versatile, with a high confidence assessment based on its detailed description and application to CHIKV.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

问题与背景

核心方法
提供的证据中并未明确说明核心的实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
