Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world.
Limitations and evidence strength
This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

巴西北部奥罗普uche热:流行病学特征、临床表现和诊断:一项整合性综述

Febre do Oropouche na Região Norte do Brasil: perfil epidemiológico, manifestações clínicas e diagnóstico: uma revisão integrativa
作者： Rafaela Kauane Costa Sousa dos Santos, Bruna de Sousa Baia, Raiane dos Santos Progenio, Andréa Cristina Monteiro dos Santos, Luiz Henrique Campos Holanda, Sleimman Augusto El Souki Cerbino, Fabíolla da Silva dos Santos, dos Santos Fabiolla da Silva
摘要中文翻译奥罗普uche热是一种新兴的虫媒病毒病,由奥罗普uche病毒(OROV)引起,该病毒属于正 Bunyavirus 属,其基因组由三个负链 RNA 片段组成。主要通过 Culicoides paraensis 虫媒传播,在巴西北部地区具有重要的流行病学意义。本研究分析了临床表现、疫情分布和鉴别诊断。采用整合性、描述性和定性分析方法,检索了 SciELO、PubMed 和 Google 学术等数据库以及官方卫生来源。纳入了 2016 年至 2026 年间发表的研究。研究结果指出,发热、头痛、肌肉痛和关节痛是主要临床表现,偶尔伴有恶心、呕吐、头晕和皮肤表现。值得注意的是,症状在初步缓解后可能复发,且出现较少见的并发症,如脑膜炎和脑膜脑炎,以及在某些特定情况下描述的出血性表现。这些发现具有临床意义,但并非该感染的特异性表现,也不足以与其他虫媒病毒病进行鉴别。自 2023 年末以来,疫情报告数量有所增加,2024 年的疫情暴发进一步凸显了亚马逊地区病毒传播的重要性。与登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热的重叠情况需要实验室确认。RT-qPCR(实时逆转录聚合酶链反应)在早期阶段有助于诊断,而血清学方法则有助于后续调查,考虑到免疫窗口期和交叉反应性。讨论部分强调了由于报告不足和诊断覆盖率差异带来的局限性。结论认为,本综述有助于识别临床表现、解读检测结果并理解区域性疫情,从而支持鉴别诊断、流行病学监测和及时应对措施,特别是在诊断资源有限的地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解奥罗普uche热在巴西亚马逊地区的流行病学特征、临床表现和诊断方法,以应对对该新兴虫媒病毒更好地理解和管理的需求。
设计与对象
进行了描述性和定性综合审查,搜索了 SciELO、PubMed 和 Google Scholar 数据库以及官方健康资源,重点关注 2016 年至 2026 年的研究。
核心方法
该综述包括奥罗普切热研究的临床和流行病学数据,采用 RT-qPCR 进行初始 诊断 和 血清学 进行后续调查。
主要结果
临床表现为发热、头痛、肌痛、关节痛,常伴有恶心、呕吐、头晕和皮肤表现。值得注意的是改善后的复发症状和不太常见的并发症,如脑膜炎和脑膜脑炎,以及特定病例的出血表现。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了识别临床表现和解释诊断测试的重要性,有助于更好地区分 诊断、流行病学 监测 以及在该地区及时做出反应。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要增强 监测 和诊断能力,特别是考虑到自 2023 年底以来通知和 暴发 的增加,强调了奥罗普切病毒的区域传播。
局限与证据强度
局限性包括报告不足和诊断覆盖范围的变化,这影响了研究结果的可靠性。由于审查的性质和缺乏随机对照试验,证据强度中等。

Febre do Oropouche na Região Norte do Brasil: perfil epidemiológico, manifestações clínicas e diagnóstico: uma revisão integrativa

Authors: Rafaela Kauane Costa Sousa dos Santos, Bruna de Sousa Baia, Raiane dos Santos Progenio, Andréa Cristina Monteiro dos Santos, Luiz Henrique Campos Holanda, Sleimman Augusto El Souki Cerbino, Fabíolla da Silva dos Santos, dos Santos Fabiolla da Silva
Original abstract (English)
A febre do Oropouche é uma arbovirose emergente causada pelo vírus Oropouche (OROV), do gênero Orthobunyavirus, com genoma constituído por três segmentos de RNA de polaridade negativa. Transmitida principalmente pelo maruim Culicoides paraensis, apresenta relevância epidemiológica na Região Norte do Brasil. Este estudo analisou manifestações clínicas, distribuição dos surtos e diagnóstico diferencial. Realizou-se uma revisão integrativa, descritiva e de abordagem qualitativa, com buscas nas bases SciELO, PubMed e Google Acadêmico e em fontes oficiais de saúde. Foram considerados estudos publicados entre 2016 e 2026. Os resultados apontam febre, cefaleia, mialgia e artralgia, acompanhadas eventualmente de náuseas, vômitos, tontura e manifestações cutâneas. Destacam-se a recorrência dos sintomas após melhora inicial e complicações menos usuais, como meningite e meningoencefalite, além de manifestações hemorrágicas descritas em algumas situações específicas. Esses achados merecem atenção clínica, embora não sejam exclusivos da infecção nem suficientes para diferenciá-la de outras arboviroses. A ampliação das notificações desde o final de 2023 e os surtos de 2024 reforçam a importância da circulação amazônica. A sobreposição com dengue, zika e chikungunya exige confirmação laboratorial. A RT-qPCR (reação em cadeia da polimerase com transcrição reversa em tempo real) contribui na fase inicial, enquanto a sorologia auxilia investigações posteriores, considerando janela imunológica e reatividade cruzada. A discussão evidencia limitações decorrentes da subnotificação e das diferenças na cobertura diagnóstica. Conclui-se que esta síntese contribui para reconhecer as manifestações clínicas, interpretar exames e compreender surtos regionais, subsidiando o diagnóstico diferencial, a vigilância epidemiológica e respostas oportunas em uma região com dificuldades de acesso diagnóstico.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the epidemiological profile, clinical manifestations, and diagnostic approach of Oropouche fever in the Brazilian Amazon region, addressing the need for better understanding and management of this emerging arbovirus.
Design and population
A descriptive and qualitative integrative review was conducted, searching SciELO, PubMed, and Google Scholar databases, and official health sources, focusing on studies from 2016 to 2026.
Core methods
The review included clinical and epidemiological data from studies on Oropouche fever, employing RT-qPCR for initial diagnosis and serology for follow-up investigations.
Main results
Clinical manifestations included fever, headache, myalgia, and arthralgia, often accompanied by nausea, vomiting, dizziness, and skin manifestations. Notable were recurrent symptoms post-improvement and less common complications like meningitis and meningoencephalitis, as well as hemorrhagic manifestations in specific cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of recognizing clinical manifestations and interpreting diagnostic tests, contributing to better differential diagnosis, epidemiological surveillance, and timely responses in the region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the need for enhanced surveillance and diagnostic capabilities, especially given the increasing notifications and outbreaks since late 2023, emphasizing the regional circulation of Oropouche virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
Limitations include underreporting and variations in diagnostic coverage, which affect the reliability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the nature of the review and the absence of randomized controlled trials.

对抗不同气候条件下的虫媒病毒事件的空间代理方案提案

PROPOSTA DE MODELO ESPAÇO-TEMPORAL PARA AVALIAR A ATUAÇÃO DOS AGENTES DE COMBATE ÀS ENDEMIAS SOBRE A INCIDÊNCIA DE ARBOVIROSES SOB DIFERENTES CONDIÇÕES CLIMÁTICAS
作者： Elaine Pereira Garrido, Erivelton Nonato de Santana, Vagner Fonseca
摘要中文翻译该研究旨在研究与防治地方病 (ACE) 和登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒后部发生的不同气候条件有关的研究。该方法结合了流行病学领域的地域性、指标、强度、连续性和解决方案、二项式负数回归和气候​​变化、空间效率和气候交互性。定义这些因果关系或后续操作的总体效果,区分关联估计和压力。嗯,因果图解是明确的,它是对警报和先前情况的回应,是昆虫学中的媒介,也是与节奏相关的复杂因素。演示、实现 60 个领土和 6.960 个观测分析的内部模拟。Nela 是一个当代积极性协会(2,79 的事件发生率),具体而言是指减少和调整 aproximou-se do efeito protetor conhecido(0,74 的估计值;0,74 的价值)。图中所示的结果并不代表 ACE 的经验证据。提议建立未来应用程序验证协议、注册资格、因果关系识别和时间和空间验证条件。

PROPOSTA DE MODELO ESPAÇO-TEMPORAL PARA AVALIAR A ATUAÇÃO DOS AGENTES DE COMBATE ÀS ENDEMIAS SOBRE A INCIDÊNCIA DE ARBOVIROSES SOB DIFERENTES CONDIÇÕES CLIMÁTICAS

Authors: Elaine Pereira Garrido, Erivelton Nonato de Santana, Vagner Fonseca
Original abstract (English)
Este artigo propõe um modelo espaço-temporal para investigar a associação entre as ações dos Agentes de Combate às Endemias (ACE) e a incidência posterior de dengue, chikungunya e Zika sob diferentes condições climáticas. O desenho teórico-metodológico combina um painel territorial por semana epidemiológica, indicadores de cobertura, intensidade, continuidade e resolutividade, regressão binomial negativa com defasagens, efeitos espaço-temporais e interações com o clima. Define-se como alvo causal o efeito total da intensificação operacional sobre casos subsequentes, distinguindo-o da associação estimada sem pressupostos adicionais. Um diagrama causal explicita a resposta das equipes aos alertas e casos prévios, o papel mediador da dinâmica entomológica e as ameaças de confundimento dependente do tempo. Como demonstração, realizou-se uma simulação inteiramente sintética com 60 territórios e 6.960 observações semanais analisadas. Nela, a associação contemporânea foi positiva (razão de incidência de 2,79), enquanto a especificação defasada e ajustada aproximou-se do efeito protetor conhecido (razão estimada de 0,74; valor gerador de 0,74). Os resultados são ilustrativos e não representam evidência empírica sobre a efetividade dos ACE. A proposta estabelece um protocolo verificável para aplicações futuras, condicionado à qualidade dos registros, à identificação causal e à validação temporal e espacial.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

虫媒病毒疾病中的母婴健康:产前登革热和 基孔肯雅病毒 感染后的临床结果。

Maternal-fetal health in arboviral diseases: clinical outcomes following prenatal dengue and chikungunya virus infections.
作者： Gielenny M. Salem, Kwan Hou Tang, Débora Familiar-Macedo, Shiao-Yng Chan, Karin Nielsen-Saines, Wei-Qiang Chen, Suan-Sin Foo, Karin Nielsen‐Saines
摘要中文翻译摘要 节肢动物传播的病毒(虫媒病毒)是全球传染病日益重要的原因,但其在怀孕和生命早期的影响仍不完全清楚。在全球生育率和出生率下降的背景下,感染对成功妊娠和新生儿健康的威胁值得更多关注。登革热病毒 (DENV) 和 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是两种最流行的蚊媒病毒,但对母婴健康的影响却未被充分认识。尽管这些感染很少导致经典的先天性综合征,但不断积累的临床、流行病学和实验证据表明,产前接触可能损害胎盘功能,导致母体免疫失调,并影响胎儿和新生儿的免疫发育。本综述总结了目前关于妊娠期间 DENV 和 CHIKV 感染对孕产妇、胎盘和儿科后果的认识。我们概述了母胎界面的免疫学特征,并讨论了妊娠相关的免疫调节如何影响 抗病毒药物 反应和胎盘稳态。然后,我们回顾了将产前虫媒病毒感染与不良妊娠结局、新生儿疾病和潜在的长期神经发育影响联系起来的流行病学和临床证据。研究了机制见解,包括病毒免疫逃避、胎盘炎症和抗体介导的免疫病理学。最后,我们讨论了监测虫媒病毒暴露的妊娠和后代的临床注意事项,以及减少孕产妇感染和改善新生儿结局的新兴策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 和登革热病毒 (DENV) 对妊娠期间母婴健康的影响。核心问题是评估产前接触这些病毒的临床结果,包括它们对胎盘功能、母亲 免疫应答 和胎儿/新生儿发育的影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述对产前登革热和 基孔肯雅病毒 感染后母婴健康结果的文献进行了系统分析。它包括临床、流行病学和实验证据,重点关注母胎界面的免疫学特征和不良结果的机制。
共识与结论
人们一致认为,产前接触登革热和 基孔肯雅病毒 会损害胎盘功能,扰乱母体免疫调节,并影响胎儿和新生儿的免疫发育。这些发现得到了不断积累的临床、流行病学和实验证据的支持。
争议与缺口
确定产前虫媒病毒感染对神经发育的长期影响的证据有限。此外,妊娠背景下的病毒免疫逃避、胎盘炎症和抗体介导的免疫病理学机制仍未得到充分探索。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要改进 监测 和对虫媒病毒暴露妊娠的监测,以及制定有针对性的策略以减少孕产妇感染和改善新生儿结局。

Maternal-fetal health in arboviral diseases: clinical outcomes following prenatal dengue and chikungunya virus infections.

Authors: Gielenny M. Salem, Kwan Hou Tang, Débora Familiar-Macedo, Shiao-Yng Chan, Karin Nielsen-Saines, Wei-Qiang Chen, Suan-Sin Foo, Karin Nielsen‐Saines
Original abstract (English)
SUMMARY Arthropod-borne viruses (arboviruses) are an increasingly important cause of global infectious disease, yet their effects during pregnancy and early life remain incompletely understood. In the context of declining fertility and birth rates globally, infectious threats to successful pregnancy and neonatal health warrant greater attention. Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), two of the most prevalent mosquito-borne viruses, are underrecognized contributors to maternal-fetal health. Although these infections rarely result in classical congenital syndromes, accumulating clinical, epidemiological, and experimental evidence indicates that prenatal exposure can impair placental function, drive maternal immune dysregulation, and influence fetal and neonatal immune development. This review summarizes current knowledge on the maternal, placental, and pediatric consequences of DENV and CHIKV infection during pregnancy. We outline the immunological features of the maternal-fetal interface and discuss how pregnancy-associated immune modulation shapes antiviral responses and placental homeostasis. We then review epidemiological and clinical evidence linking prenatal arboviral infection with adverse pregnancy outcomes, neonatal disease, and potential long-term neurodevelopmental effects. Mechanistic insights, including viral immune evasion, placental inflammation, and antibody-mediated immunopathology, are examined. Finally, we discuss clinical considerations for monitoring arboviral-exposed pregnancies and progenies, alongside emerging strategies to reduce maternal infection and improve neonatal outcomes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the effects of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and dengue virus (DENV) on maternal-fetal health during pregnancy. The central question is to evaluate the clinical outcomes of prenatal exposure to these viruses, including their impact on placental function, maternal immune responses, and fetal/neonatal development.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of the literature on maternal-fetal health outcomes following prenatal dengue and chikungunya virus infections. It includes clinical, epidemiological, and experimental evidence, with a focus on the immunological features of the maternal-fetal interface and the mechanisms underlying adverse outcomes.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that prenatal exposure to dengue and chikungunya viruses can impair placental function, disrupt maternal immune regulation, and affect fetal and neonatal immune development. These findings are supported by accumulating clinical, epidemiological, and experimental evidence.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited in its ability to establish long-term neurodevelopmental effects of prenatal arboviral infections. Additionally, the mechanisms of viral immune evasion, placental inflammation, and antibody-mediated immunopathology remain underexplored in the context of pregnancy.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for improved surveillance and monitoring of arboviral-exposed pregnancies, as well as the development of targeted strategies to reduce maternal infection and enhance neonatal outcomes.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

基孔肯雅病毒关节炎的多模态生物标志物:临床表型分析与个性化治疗研究议程

Multimodal biomarkers in chikungunya arthritis: clinical phenotyping and a research agenda for personalized therapy
作者： Ruifeng Lin, Wenxia Lin, Jiabao Xu, Yangtao Liu, Hongjie Zhang, Xiaoling Chen, Hanbin Liu, Siqi Zhang, Qingqing Zhang, Jiani Liu
摘要中文翻译背景 基孔肯雅病毒关节炎包含以疼痛为主、关节周围、炎症性和类风湿性关节炎(RA)等多种临床表型。单纯症状持续时间无法反映这种异质性。范围 这篇叙述性综述评估了病毒学检测、抗病毒药物和自身免疫检测、炎症介质、影像学检查和临床结果。人类观察结果与动物和细胞机制区分开来。发现 大多数生物标志物证据仍处于探索阶段。与并发症状的关联必须与预测后期疾病以及预测不同抗病毒药物治疗效果区分开来。小样本量、异质性检测和采样时间、抗病毒药物暴露以及有限的外部验证限制了结果的解释。持续存在的抗病毒药物抗体或孤立的细胞因子升高并不能识别出经过验证的抗病毒药物反应亚组。组织中的病毒RNA或抗原无法确定活跃感染或持续症状的病因。临床表型是可观察的状态;提出的机制性内型尚未得到验证。意义 当前评估应整合疾病阶段、客观炎症、功能负担、共病和替代诊断。甲氨蝶呤的证据受限于联合用药、未受控的观察以及小型比较试验。抗病毒药物、中和抗体和靶向通路的免疫调节剂在基孔肯雅病毒关节炎中仍处于研究阶段。个性化抗病毒药物治疗是一个需要纵向抗病毒药物抗体检测和前瞻性生物标志物-抗病毒药物相互作用试验的研究议程,而不是目前可用的生物标志物导向策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
抗病毒药物、基孔肯雅病毒疾病和已知的基孔肯雅病毒谱系。综述重点在于基孔肯雅病毒关节炎的多模态生物标志物,特别强调临床表型分析和个性化治疗的研究议程。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述评估了病毒学检测、抗病毒药物和自身免疫检测、炎症介质、影像学检查和临床结果。综述未报告具体数据库、检索日期或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
大多数生物标志物证据仍处于探索阶段,临床表型是可观察的状态;提出的机制性内型尚未得到验证。持续存在的抗病毒药物抗体和孤立的细胞因子升高并不能识别出经过验证的抗病毒药物反应亚组。
争议与缺口
单纯症状持续时间无法反映基孔肯雅病毒关节炎的异质性。甲氨蝶呤的证据受限于联合用药、未受控的观察以及小型比较试验。组织中的病毒RNA或抗原无法确定活跃感染或持续症状的病因。
科研与实践启示
个性化抗病毒药物治疗是一个需要纵向抗病毒药物抗体检测和前瞻性生物标志物-抗病毒药物相互作用试验的研究议程,而不是目前可用的生物标志物导向策略。与并发症状的关联必须与预测后期疾病以及预测不同抗病毒药物治疗效果区分开来。当前评估应整合疾病阶段、客观炎症、功能负担、共病和替代诊断。

Multimodal biomarkers in chikungunya arthritis: clinical phenotyping and a research agenda for personalized therapy

Authors: Ruifeng Lin, Wenxia Lin, Jiabao Xu, Yangtao Liu, Hongjie Zhang, Xiaoling Chen, Hanbin Liu, Siqi Zhang, Qingqing Zhang, Jiani Liu
Original abstract (English)
Background Chikungunya arthritis comprises overlapping pain-dominant, periarticular, inflammatory, and rheumatoid arthritis (RA)-like clinical phenotypes. Symptom duration alone does not capture this heterogeneity. Scope This narrative review evaluates virological assays, antiviral and autoimmune serology, inflammatory mediators, imaging, and clinical outcomes. Human observations are distinguished from animal and cell-based mechanisms. Findings Most biomarker evidence is exploratory. Associations with concurrent symptoms must be distinguished from prediction of later disease and from prediction of differential treatment benefit. Small samples, heterogeneous assays and sampling times, treatment exposure, and limited external validation restrict interpretation. Neither persistent antiviral antibodies nor an isolated cytokine elevation identifies a validated treatment-responsive subgroup. Tissue viral RNA or antigen does not establish productive infection or a causal explanation for persistent symptoms. Clinical phenotypes are observable states; proposed mechanistic endotypes remain unvalidated. Implications Current assessment should integrate disease phase, objective inflammation, functional burden, comorbidity, and alternative diagnoses. Evidence for methotrexate is limited by combination regimens, uncontrolled observations, and small comparative trials. Antivirals, neutralizing antibodies, and pathway-targeted immunomodulators remain investigational for CHIKV arthritis. Personalized treatment is a research agenda requiring longitudinal cohorts and prospective biomarker-by-treatment interaction trials, rather than a currently available biomarker-directed strategy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chikungunya virus, chikungunya disease and recognized CHIKV lineages. The review focuses on multimodal biomarkers in chikungunya arthritis, with an emphasis on clinical phenotyping and a research agenda for personalized therapy.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review evaluates virological assays, antiviral and autoimmune serology, inflammatory mediators, imaging, and clinical outcomes. The review does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
Most biomarker evidence is exploratory, and clinical phenotypes are observable states; proposed mechanistic endotypes remain unvalidated. Persistent antiviral antibodies and isolated cytokine elevation do not identify a validated treatment-responsive subgroup.
Controversies and gaps
Symptom duration alone does not capture the heterogeneity of chikungunya arthritis. Evidence for methotrexate is limited by combination regimens, uncontrolled observations, and small comparative trials. Tissue viral RNA or antigen does not establish productive infection or a causal explanation for persistent symptoms.
Research and practice implications
Personalized treatment is a research agenda requiring longitudinal cohorts and prospective biomarker-by-treatment interaction trials, rather than a currently available biomarker-directed strategy. Associations with concurrent symptoms must be distinguished from prediction of later disease and from prediction of differential treatment benefit. Current assessment should integrate disease phase, objective inflammation, functional burden, comorbidity, and alternative diagnoses.

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas Urbanas: revisión sistemática 城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热的流行病学影响:系统评价

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Controversies and gaps
La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión.

Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado.

基于微小RNA的现场快速诊断方法用于新发和再发传染病:从生物标志物发现到临床应用

MicroRNA-based point-of-care diagnostics for emerging and re-emerging infectious diseases: from biomarker discovery to clinical implementation.
作者： Victoria Cappleman, Himanshu Gupta
摘要中文翻译引言:微小RNA(miRNA)作为感染性疾病的免疫应答生物标志物正受到越来越多的关注,但将差异表达研究转化为可临床部署的诊断方法仍存在局限。研究领域:本综述评估了miRNA在疟疾、登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒感染、克里米亚-刚果出血热、日本脑炎和尼帕病毒感染中的诊断和转化潜力。与以往主要关注生物标志物发现或检测技术的综述不同,本文提供了跨疾病的转化视角,将miRNA特征与分析和临床验证、现场快速诊断开发、临床应用、经济考量以及资源匮乏地区的部署联系起来。专家观点:miRNA诊断的下一步应优先考虑可重复的多miRNA特征、标准化的预分析流程以及前瞻性多中心验证,而不是继续单独进行生物标志物发现。临床采用将取决于将这些特征整合到经济实惠的样本到答案平台中,并在真实世界条件下证明其结果能够改善特定的临床决策。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),特别是东/中/南非(ECSA)和亚洲谱系,并探讨了基于微小RNA(miRNA)的诊断方法在检测和临床管理基孔肯雅热、基孔肯雅病毒感染和慢性基孔肯雅关节炎中的应用。核心问题是:基于miRNA的现场快速诊断方法是否能够有效地从生物标志物发现转化为这些疾病的临床应用。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对CHIKV的miRNA诊断方法的系统性评估,涵盖多个谱系及相关疾病。它包括跨疾病的转化视角,将miRNA特征与分析和临床验证、现场快速诊断开发以及经济考量联系起来。综述并未明确报告元分析或汇总估计,但强调了将miRNA特征整合到诊断平台中的重要性。
共识与结论
综述强调,基于miRNA的诊断方法在CHIKV感染中具有临床部署的潜力,特别是在整合到经济实惠的样本到答案平台中时。它突出了开发可重复的多miRNA特征和标准化的预分析流程对于提高诊断准确性和可靠性的重要性。
争议与缺口
在CHIKV中,基于miRNA的诊断方法缺乏标准化的预分析流程和前瞻性多中心验证。此外,综述指出,尽管miRNA特征显示出潜力,但其临床实用性在真实世界环境中尚未得到证实,需要进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑开发可重复的多miRNA特征、标准化的预分析流程以及前瞻性多中心验证,以增强基于miRNA的诊断方法在CHIKV中的临床实用性。这些努力对于将miRNA研究转化为资源匮乏地区的实用、经济的诊断工具至关重要。

MicroRNA-based point-of-care diagnostics for emerging and re-emerging infectious diseases: from biomarker discovery to clinical implementation.

Authors: Victoria Cappleman, Himanshu Gupta
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: MicroRNAs (miRNAs) are increasingly investigated as host-response biomarkers for infectious diseases, but translation from differential-expression studies to clinically deployable diagnostics remains limited. AREAS COVERED: This review evaluates the diagnostic and translational potential of miRNAs across malaria, dengue, chikungunya, Zika virus infection, Crimean-Congo hemorrhagic fever, Japanese encephalitis and Nipah virus disease. Unlike prior reviews focused mainly on biomarker discovery or detection technologies, it provides a cross-disease translational perspective linking miRNA signatures with analytical and clinical validation, point-of-care development, clinical implementation, economic considerations and deployment in resource-limited settings. EXPERT OPINION: The next phase of miRNA diagnostics should prioritize reproducible multi-miRNA signatures, standardized pre-analytical workflows and prospective multicentre validation rather than continued biomarker discovery alone. Clinical adoption will depend on integrating these signatures into affordable sample-to-answer platforms and demonstrating that results improve defined clinical decisions under real-world conditions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), specifically the East/Central/South African (ECSA) and Asian lineages, and examines the use of microRNA (miRNA)-based diagnostics for the detection and clinical management of chikungunya fever, chikungunya infection, and chronic chikungunya arthritis. The central question is whether miRNA-based point-of-care diagnostics can be effectively translated from biomarker discovery to clinical implementation for these conditions.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic evaluation of miRNA-based diagnostics for CHIKV, covering multiple lineages and related diseases. It includes a cross-disease translational perspective, linking miRNA signatures with analytical and clinical validation, point-of-care development, and economic considerations. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but emphasizes the integration of miRNA signatures into diagnostic platforms.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that miRNA-based diagnostics have potential for clinical deployment in CHIKV infections, particularly when integrated into affordable sample-to-answer platforms. It underscores the importance of reproducible multi-miRNA signatures and standardized pre-analytical workflows for improving diagnostic accuracy and reliability.
Controversies and gaps
There is a lack of standardized pre-analytical workflows and prospective multicentre validation for miRNA-based diagnostics in CHIKV. Additionally, the review notes that while miRNA signatures show promise, their clinical utility remains unproven in real-world settings, necessitating further research.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize the development of reproducible multi-miRNA signatures, standardized pre-analytical workflows, and prospective multicentre validation to enhance the clinical utility of miRNA-based diagnostics for CHIKV. These efforts are critical for translating miRNA research into practical, affordable diagnostic tools for resource-limited settings.

印度儿童急性脑炎综合征的病因谱:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
摘要中文翻译背景:急性脑炎综合征(AES)仍然是印度儿童患病和死亡的重要原因。虽然日本脑炎(JE)病毒传统上被认为是主要原因,但最近的研究表明,致病微生物的范围已大大扩大。目的:估计印度 18 岁或以下儿童中由各种常见感染病因引起的 AES 病例的汇总比例。方法:对 PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE 和 Scopus 进行全面检索,查找 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的研究。在印度进行的研究,包括至少 25 名患有 AES 的儿童(≤ 18 岁),并报告了实验室确认的病因,被认为是合格的。病例报告 和小箱系列被排除在外。使用乔安娜布里格斯研究所清单对纳入的研究进行质量评估。使用随机效应模型来获得汇总估计值及其相应的 95% 置信区间 (CI)。结果:共有69项研究纳入最终分析。恙虫病是最常见的病因,占 AES 病例的 20%(95%CI:13%-27%),其次是乙脑,占 13%(95%CI:11%-15%)。肠道病毒占病例的 6%(95%CI:3%-9%)。登革热、单纯疱疹病毒和 Epstein-Barr 病毒各占病例的 5%(95%CI:分别为 3%-8%、2%-9% 和 1%-11%)。腮腺炎和 基孔肯雅病毒 各占 4%(95%CI:分别为 1%-8% 和 2%-5%)。结论:恙虫病和乙型脑炎是印度儿童 AES 的主要可识别病因。更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可以提高 AES 儿童的诊断率并促进早期靶向治疗。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点探讨印度儿童急性脑炎综合征(AES)的病因谱,特别强调确定各种感染性病因在病例中的比例,包括基孔肯雅病毒。
证据基础与方法
对PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE和Scopus进行了全面检索,收集了2000年1月至2025年12月期间发表的研究。排除了病例报告和小型病例系列。使用Joanna Briggs研究所清单对纳入研究进行了质量评估。采用随机效应模型以获得合并估计值及其对应的95%置信区间(CI)。
共识与结论
恙虫病和日本脑炎 (JE) 是印度儿科 AES 的主要可识别病因。基孔肯雅病毒 占病例的 4%(95%CI:2%-5%)。
争议与缺口
综述指出,AES的致病病原体范围已显著扩大,表明日本脑炎(JE)可能不再是唯一的首要病因。然而,证据中并未报告与基孔肯雅病毒相关的具体争议或证据缺口。
科研与实践启示
更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可能会提高AES的诊断率,并有助于对儿童AES实施早期针对性治疗。综述建议需要进一步研究以更好地了解基孔肯雅病毒在AES中的作用,并提高诊断能力。

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) continues to be an important cause of illness and death among children in India. While Japanese encephalitis (JE) virus has traditionally been regarded as the leading cause, recent studies indicate that the range of causative organisms has widened considerably. AIM: To estimate the pooled proportion of AES cases attributable to various common infectious etiologies among children aged 18 years or younger in India. METHODS: A comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus was conducted for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. Studies conducted in India that included at least 25 children (≤ 18 years) with AES and reported laboratory-confirmed etiologies were considered eligible. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI). RESULTS: A total of 69 studies were included in the final analysis. Scrub typhus emerged as the most common cause, accounting for 20% (95%CI: 13%-27%) of AES cases, followed by JE at 13% (95%CI: 11%-15%). Enterovirus accounted for 6% of the cases (95%CI: 3%-9%). Dengue, herpes simplex virus and Epstein-Barr virus accounted for 5% of the cases each (95%CI: 3%-8%, 2%-9%, and 1%-11%, respectively). Mumps and chikungunya virus accounted for 4% each (95%CI: 1%-8%, and 2%-5%, respectively). CONCLUSION: Scrub typhus and JE are the leading identifiable etiology of pediatric AES in India. Broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) in children in India, with a specific emphasis on identifying the proportion of cases attributable to various infectious etiologies, including chikungunya virus.
Evidence base and method
A comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus was conducted for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI).
Consensus and conclusions
Scrub typhus and Japanese encephalitis (JE) are the leading identifiable etiologies of pediatric AES in India. Chikungunya virus accounted for 4% of the cases (95%CI: 2%-5%).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that the range of causative organisms for AES has widened considerably, suggesting that JE may no longer be the sole leading cause. However, the evidence does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to chikungunya virus.
Research and practice implications
Broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES. The review suggests that further research is needed to better understand the role of chikungunya virus in AES and to improve diagnostic capabilities.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

植物提取物对印度地区登革热和白纹伊蚊的幼虫杀灭活性评估:综述

Assessment of the Larvicidal Activity of Plant Extracts, with Special Reference to Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus in India: A Review
作者： Kaliyamoorthy Dass, S. Prabhu, Rangarajan Murugesan
摘要中文翻译蚊子是传播多种致命疾病(如基孔肯雅热、登革热、丝虫病、出血热、日本脑炎、疟疾、黄热病、西尼罗河病毒和寨卡热)的媒介。每年有数百万人受到蚊媒疾病的影响。因此,蚊虫控制对于公共卫生至关重要,尤其是在热带地区。各种合成化学物质,如拟除虫菊酯、派卡瑞丁、邻苯二甲酸二甲酯、碳酸二甲酯、有机磷和氨基甲酸酯,广泛用于蚊虫控制。这些化合物可以根据其作用机制分为杀虫剂(如拟除虫菊酯、有机磷和氨基甲酸酯)和驱虫剂(如派卡瑞丁和邻苯二甲酸二甲酯)。然而,它们的广泛使用与一些不良影响相关,包括生物累积、环境污染、持久性(不可生物降解)以及对非目标生物的毒性。为克服这些影响,我们必须寻找替代方案。相反,基于植物的植物化学物质由于其高效性、环境安全性和作为合成幼虫杀灭剂的可行替代品的适用性,在蚊虫控制计划中占据主导地位。本综述提供了证据表明,多种植物提取物可以控制白纹伊蚊种群。这种控制对于减少蚊媒疾病的传播至关重要。通过探索各种当地可获得的植物提取物,本研究突出了研究空白,指导未来的研究方向,并促进环保的媒介管理策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于植物提取物对埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的幼虫杀灭活性,这些蚊子是传播基孔肯雅热和其他蚊媒疾病的重要媒介。核心问题是:基于植物的替代方案是否能够有效控制这些蚊虫种群,从而减少疾病传播。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对用于蚊虫控制的植物提取物进行系统分析,特别强调其对埃及伊蚊属物种的有效性。它没有明确报告所搜索的数据库、纳入标准或元分析方法,但包含了对该主题的一般讨论及其对公共卫生的相关性。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,植物提取物在控制白纹伊蚊种群方面是有效的,这对于减少蚊媒疾病的传播至关重要。它强调了这些提取物作为合成幼虫杀灭剂的可行替代品的潜力,因为它们具有环境安全性和高效性。
争议与缺口
综述指出,尽管基于植物的植物化学物质高效且环境安全,但仍需要更多研究来探索它们对其他蚊虫物种的效果,并填补对其长期生态影响理解上的空白。
科研与实践启示
综述建议未来的研究应专注于识别和测试更多当地可获得的植物提取物的幼虫杀灭活性。它还强调了推广环保的媒介管理策略的重要性,以减轻合成化学物质的不良影响。}}.{

Assessment of the Larvicidal Activity of Plant Extracts, with Special Reference to Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus in India: A Review

Authors: Kaliyamoorthy Dass, S. Prabhu, Rangarajan Murugesan
Original abstract (English)
Mosquitoes are vectors for various deadly diseases, such as chikungunya, dengue fever, filariasis, haemorrhagic fever, Japanese encephalitis, malaria, yellow fever, the West Nile virus, and Zika fever. Mosquito-borne diseases affect millions of people every year. Consequently, mosquito control is essential for public health, particularly in tropical areas. Various synthetic chemicals, such as pyrethrum, picaridin, dimethyl phthalate, dimethyl carbonate, organophosphates, and carbamates, are widely used in mosquito control. These compounds can be broadly classified into insecticides (e.g., pyrethrum, organophosphates, and carbamates) and insect repellents (e.g., picaridin and dimethyl phthalate) based on their mode of action. However, their extensive use is associated with adverse effects, including biomagnification, environmental pollution, persistence (non-biodegradability), and toxicity to non-target organisms. To overcome these effects, we must seek alternatives. Conversely, plant-based phytochemicals are predominantly active in mosquito control initiatives due to their efficiency, environmental safety, and suitability as a viable alternative to synthetic larvicides. This review provides evidence that various plant extracts can control Ae. albopictus mosquito populations. This control is crucial for reducing the incidence of mosquito-borne diseases. By exploring various locally available plant extracts, this study highlights research gaps, guides future studies, and promotes eco-friendly vector management.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the larvicidal activity of plant extracts against Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes, which are vectors for chikungunya and other mosquito-borne diseases. The central question is whether plant-based alternatives can effectively control these mosquito populations to reduce disease transmission.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of plant extracts used in mosquito control, with an emphasis on their efficacy against Aedes species. It does not explicitly report on the databases searched, eligibility criteria, or meta-analysis methods, but it includes a general discussion of the topic and its relevance to public health.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that plant extracts can be effective in controlling Ae. albopictus populations, which is crucial for reducing the incidence of mosquito-borne diseases. It highlights the potential of these extracts as a viable alternative to synthetic larvicides due to their environmental safety and efficiency.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while plant-based phytochemicals are efficient and environmentally safe, there is a need for more research to explore their effectiveness against other mosquito species and to address gaps in understanding their long-term impact on ecosystems.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on identifying and testing more locally available plant extracts for their larvicidal activity. It also emphasizes the importance of promoting eco-friendly vector management strategies to mitigate the adverse effects of synthetic chemicals.

分子动力学模拟在疫苗设计中的应用:用于虫媒病毒

Application of Molecular Dynamics Simulations in Vaccine Design for Arboviruses
作者： Day‐Yu Chao
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒由三个病毒家族组成,是一类通过昆虫传播的多样化病毒。尽管大多数虫媒病毒感染仍保持人兽共患病特性,并在森林生态循环中持续存在,但一些病毒,如登革病毒(DENV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),已经适应了城市循环,以人类为主要扩增疫苗,导致严重的医疗后果。尽管针对黄热病毒的减毒活疫苗(LA 疫苗)在提供保护性免疫方面已被证明是成功的,但针对DENV和CHIKV的其他LA 疫苗的安全性问题仍然存在。包膜病毒样颗粒(eVLPs)已成为虫媒病毒疫苗开发的领先平台,包括登革病毒、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒,因为它们能够重现真实病毒的抗原表面,而不会带来传染风险。然而,大多数eVLPs缺乏已解析的结构,且包膜蛋白与脂质双层之间的相互作用仍不清楚,导致关于其组装、稳定性和免疫原性的关键问题尚未解决。本综述探讨了分子动力学(MD)模拟如何与冷冻电镜结合,用于解决这些缺口。结构比较显示,黄病毒的eVLPs通常采用T = 1二十面体对称结构,该结构由与T = 3病毒相同的不对称prM-E三聚体构建而成。对登革病毒eVLPs的MD研究揭示了E-M蛋白簇如何作为膜弯曲单元,以及如何通过计算机模拟的脂质面残基突变,转化为实验验证的分泌增强型eVLP变体。分子动力学模拟已从一种描述性工具发展为理性eVLP工程的主动驱动力,其与冷冻电镜、人工智能加速的力场和增强采样方法的持续整合,对于下一代虫媒病毒疫苗设计至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)及其相关疾病,包括关节痛(基孔肯雅热)、基孔肯雅病毒感染和慢性基孔肯雅关节炎。核心问题是:分子动力学(MD)模拟如何用于解决基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs设计中的未解问题,特别是关于其组装、稳定性和免疫原性。
证据基础与方法
本综述基于基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs的分子动力学模拟,这些模拟与冷冻电镜相结合。这些模拟涵盖全原子和粗粒化分辨率,并属于多尺度工作流程的一部分。综述未报告特定数据库、搜索日期或纳入研究的资格标准。
共识与结论
分子动力学模拟表明,基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs中的E-M蛋白簇在膜弯曲中起关键作用。对脂质面残基进行计算机模拟突变已产生实验验证的分泌增强型eVLP变体。这些发现突显了分子动力学模拟在理解并改进基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs设计中的重要性。
争议与缺口
大多数基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs缺乏已解析的结构,且包膜蛋白与脂质双层之间的相互作用仍不清楚。这些未解问题阻碍了对eVLPs组装、稳定性和免疫原性的全面理解,导致其功能和在疫苗开发中的应用存在关键疑问。
科研与实践启示
将分子动力学模拟与冷冻电镜、人工智能加速的力场和增强采样方法整合,对于推进下一代虫媒病毒疫苗至关重要。这种方法将有助于解决基孔肯雅病毒eVLPs中的未解结构和功能缺口,从而支持更有效的疫苗设计和开发。

Application of Molecular Dynamics Simulations in Vaccine Design for Arboviruses

Authors: Day‐Yu Chao
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses, which comprise three virus families, are a diverse group of viruses transmitted by insects. While most arboviral diseases remain zoonotic and persist in a sylvatic cycle, some viruses, such as dengue (DENV) and chikungunya (CHIKV), have adapted to an urban cycle, with humans as the primary amplification host, causing significant medical consequences. While the live-attenuated (LA) vaccine against yellow fever virus has proven successful in providing protective immunity, safety concerns about other LA vaccines against DENV and CHIKV remain. Enveloped virus-like particles (eVLPs) have emerged as a leading vaccine platform for arboviruses, including dengue, Zika, and chikungunya, because they recapitulate the antigenic surface of authentic virions without infectious risk. Yet most eVLPs lack solved structures, and the interaction between the envelope protein and the lipid bilayers remains unknown, leaving key questions about assembly, stability, and immunogenicity unresolved. This review examines how molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, spanning all-atom and coarse-grained resolutions and increasingly deployed in multiscale workflows, are being integrated with cryo-electron microscopy to address these gaps. Structural comparisons show that flavivirus eVLPs typically adopt T = 1 icosahedral symmetry built from the same asymmetric prM-E trimer that organizes T = 3 virions. MD studies of dengue eVLPs reveal how E-M protein clusters act as membrane-bending units, and how in silico mutagenesis of lipid-facing residues can be translated into experimentally validated, secretion-enhancing eVLP variants. MD simulation has matured from a descriptive tool into an active driver of rational eVLP engineering, and its continued integration with cryo-EM, AI-accelerated force fields, and enhanced sampling methods will be essential for next-generation arbovirus vaccine design.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and its associated diseases, including chikungunya fever, chikungunya infection, and chronic chikungunya arthritis. The central question is how molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can be used to address unresolved issues in the design of envelope virus-like particles (eVLPs) for CHIKV, particularly regarding their assembly, stability, and immunogenicity.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on MD simulations of CHIKV eVLPs, which are integrated with cryo-electron microscopy. These simulations span all-atom and coarse-grained resolutions and are part of multiscale workflows. The review does not report on specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria for included studies.
Consensus and conclusions
MD simulations have demonstrated that the E-M protein clusters in CHIKV eVLPs function as membrane-bending units. In silico mutagenesis of lipid-facing residues has led to experimentally validated eVLP variants with enhanced secretion. These findings highlight the utility of MD simulations in understanding and improving the design of eVLPs for CHIKV.
Controversies and gaps
Most eVLPs for CHIKV lack solved structures, and the interaction between the envelope protein and the lipid bilayers remains unclear. These unresolved aspects hinder a full understanding of eVLP assembly, stability, and immunogenicity, leaving key questions about their functionality and application in vaccine development.
Research and practice implications
The integration of MD simulations with cryo-EM, AI-accelerated force fields, and enhanced sampling methods is essential for advancing next-generation arbovirus vaccines. This approach will help address the unresolved structural and functional gaps in CHIKV eVLPs, supporting more effective vaccine design and development.

气候塑造非洲蚊媒疾病传播:对气候决定因素、生物反应和传播动态的综述

Climate shapes mosquito-borne disease transmission in africa: a scoping review of climatic determinants, biological responses and transmission dynamics
作者： Emanuel Catumbela, Wison Nelson Manuel Gonga, Semedo Faria
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 蚊媒疾病仍然是非洲的主要公共卫生挑战,其传播受气候条件的强烈影响。然而,现有证据在地理分布和方法学上存在不均衡和异质性。目的 映射并综合非洲蚊媒疾病传播受温度、降雨/降水量和相对湿度影响的证据,并比较主要媒介-病原体系统中的气候模式。方法 采用PRISMA-ScR框架进行综述。纳入标准为:在非洲进行或提供可识别的非洲特定证据的同行评审原始研究,研究对象包括疟疾、登革热、基孔肯雅热或寨卡病毒,并评估温度、降雨/降水量和/或相对湿度与生物、媒介、生态、传播相关或流行病学结果的关系。结果 共纳入18项原始研究:10项关于疟疾,6项关于登革热,2项关于寨卡病毒;没有专门针对基孔肯雅热的研究。温度是最常评估的暴露因素。疟疾研究显示温度与传播的关系是非线性和情境依赖的,包括在接近25 °C时出现疟疾阳性率的峰值。登革热研究显示温度信号的异质性,包括在约21 °C以上时温度与传播的关系更强,而在特定环境下,29–30 °C时传播相关结果增加。降雨通常会增加媒介的丰度或疟疾的传播,但过量降雨有时会降低幼虫存活率或登革热的传播。相对湿度表现出情境依赖效应,包括Aedes aegypti存活与寨卡病毒感染之间的权衡。报告的气候滞后时间主要在1至4个月之间。结论 非洲蚊媒疾病传播的气候影响是非线性的、疾病特异性的和情境依赖的。需要更强的气候知情监测和本地化的整合研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于映射和综合温度、降雨/降水量和相对湿度对非洲蚊媒疾病传播的影响证据,特别强调基孔肯雅病毒。核心问题是:气候因素如何影响该地区基孔肯雅病毒及其相关疾病传播的动态?
证据基础与方法
采用PRISMA-ScR框架进行综述。证据基础包括在非洲进行或提供可识别的非洲特定证据的同行评审原始研究,研究对象包括疟疾、登革热、基孔肯雅热或寨卡病毒,并评估温度、降雨/降水量和/或相对湿度与生物、媒介、生态、传播相关或流行病学结果的关系。
共识与结论
综述得出结论:非洲蚊媒疾病传播的气候影响是非线性的、疾病特异性的和情境依赖的。温度是最常评估的暴露因素,其影响在不同疾病间存在差异,疟疾表现出非线性和情境依赖的关系,登革热表现出异质的温度信号,而降雨通常会增加媒介的丰度或疟疾的传播。
争议与缺口
现有证据在地理分布和方法学上存在不均衡和异质性。缺乏专门针对基孔肯雅热的研究,且报告的气候滞后时间主要在1至4个月之间,表明对气候因素影响传播的精确时间仍有理解上的空白。
科研与实践启示
需要更强的气候知情监测和本地化的整合研究,以更好地理解和应对非洲基孔肯雅病毒的传播动态。

Climate shapes mosquito-borne disease transmission in africa: a scoping review of climatic determinants, biological responses and transmission dynamics

Authors: Emanuel Catumbela, Wison Nelson Manuel Gonga, Semedo Faria
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Mosquito-borne diseases remain a major public-health challenge in Africa, and their transmission is strongly influenced by climatic conditions. However, the evidence is geographically uneven and methodologically heterogeneous. Objective To map and synthesise evidence on the influence of temperature, rainfall/precipitation and relative humidity on mosquito-borne disease transmission in Africa, and to compare climatic patterns across major vector–pathogen systems. Methods A scoping review was conducted following PRISMA-ScR. Peer-reviewed primary studies conducted in Africa, or providing identifiable Africa-specific evidence, were included if they investigated malaria, dengue, chikungunya or Zika and assessed temperature, rainfall/precipitation and/or relative humidity in relation to biological, entomological, ecological, transmission-related or epidemiological outcomes. Results Eighteen primary studies were included: 10 on malaria, 6 on dengue and 2 on Zika; none specifically addressed chikungunya. Temperature was the most frequently assessed exposure. Malaria studies showed non-linear and context-dependent temperature relationships, including a field peak in malaria positivity near 25 °C. Dengue studies showed heterogeneous temperature signals, including stronger temperature–incidence relationships above approximately 21 °C and increased transmission-related outcomes around 29–30 °C in specific settings. Rainfall generally increased vector abundance or malaria transmission, although excessive rainfall sometimes reduced larval survival or dengue transmission. Relative humidity showed context-dependent effects, including trade-offs between Aedes aegypti survival and Zika virus infection. Reported climatic lags ranged mainly from one to four months. Conclusion Climatic influences on mosquito-borne disease transmission in Africa are non-linear, disease-specific and context-dependent. Stronger climate-informed surveillance and locally resolved integrated research are needed.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on mapping and synthesising evidence on the influence of temperature, rainfall/precipitation and relative humidity on mosquito-borne disease transmission in Africa, with a specific emphasis on Chikungunya virus. The central question is how climatic factors affect the transmission dynamics of Chikungunya virus and its associated disease in the region.
Evidence base and method
A scoping review was conducted following PRISMA-ScR. The evidence base includes peer-reviewed primary studies conducted in Africa or providing identifiable Africa-specific evidence that investigated malaria, dengue, chikungunya or Zika and assessed temperature, rainfall/precipitation and/or relative humidity in relation to biological, entomological, ecological, transmission-related or epidemiological outcomes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that climatic influences on mosquito-borne disease transmission in Africa are non-linear, disease-specific and context-dependent. Temperature was the most frequently assessed exposure, and its effects varied across diseases, with malaria showing non-linear and context-dependent relationships, dengue showing heterogeneous temperature signals, and rainfall generally increasing vector abundance or malaria transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is geographically uneven and methodologically heterogeneous. There is a lack of specific studies addressing chikungunya, and the reported climatic lags ranged mainly from one to four months, indicating potential gaps in understanding the precise timing of climatic effects on transmission.
Research and practice implications
Stronger climate-informed surveillance and locally resolved integrated research are needed to better understand and address the transmission dynamics of Chikungunya virus in Africa.

欧盟/欧洲经济区 基孔肯雅病毒 疾病的季节性 监测 – 2026 – 每周

Seasonal surveillance of chikungunya virus disease in the EU/EEA – 2026 – Weekly
新闻简报欧洲疾病预防和控制中心(ECDC)发布了截至 2026 年 11 月 13 日关于欧盟/欧洲经济区 监测 疾病的每周季节性 监测 报告。报告显示,欧洲两个国家报告了本地感染病例:法国有 213 例,意大利有 1 例。自上次更新以来,法国新增了六例新病例,而意大利尚未报告任何新病例。这些病例分布在 31 个聚集性感染群中,其中 14 个聚集性病例目前处于活动状态,其中最大的聚集性病例位于普拉萨克(38 例)和滨海卡瓦莱尔(34 例)。值得注意的是,瑞士报告了提契诺州的四例本地感染病例,这是该国首例此类病例。然而,这些数据并未包含在 ECDC 数据中,因为瑞士不向该机构报告。提契诺州新发现的感染群已经关闭。ECDC 将继续监测情况并发布每周报告,直至 2026 年 11 月。
查看新闻五要素
时间
自2026年初至2026年11月11日(N1、N2、N16、N17、N18)。
地点与对象
法国(213例)、意大利(1例)、瑞士(提契诺州4例,未包含在ECDC数据中)(N1,N2,N10,N11,N12).
事件
关于欧盟/欧洲经济区 监测 疾病的季节性 监测 报告,指出法国和意大利本地获得的病例,以及瑞士的首例病例(N1、N2、N10、N11)。
规模、影响与风险
法国213例,意大利1例,瑞士4例(未包含在ECDC数据中)。31个集群,其中14个集群处于活跃状态(N1,N2,N4,N6,N7,N8,N10,N11,N12)。
应对、状态与不确定性
ECDC 将继续监测情况并发布每周报告,直至 2026 年 11 月。瑞士新发现的集群被视为已关闭(N13、N15、N5、N9)。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Seasonal surveillance of chikungunya virus disease in the EU/EEA – 2026 – Weekly

News Brief
The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) has released a weekly seasonal surveillance report on chikungunya virus disease in the EU/EEA as of 13 November 2026. The report indicates that two countries in Europe have reported locally acquired cases: France with 213 cases and Italy with one case. Since the last update, France has added six new cases, while Italy has not reported any new cases. The cases are distributed across 31 clusters, with 14 currently active, including the largest in Prayssac (38 cases) and Cavalaire-sur-Mer (34 cases). Notably, Switzerland has reported four locally acquired cases in Ticino, marking the first such cases in the country. However, these are not included in ECDC data as Switzerland does not report to the agency. The newly identified cluster in Ticino is already closed. ECDC will continue monitoring the situation and publish weekly reports until November 2026.

strengthening collaboration, preparedness & response Home Public health events Seasonal surveillance of chikungunya virus disease in the EU/EEA – 2026 – Weekly 13 November 2026 Seasonal surveillance of chikungunya virus disease in the EU/EEA – 2026 – Weekly Periodic monitoring Active 13 November 2026 Key facts Since the beginning of 2026 and as at 11 November 2026, two countries in Europe have reported locally acquired cases of chikungunya virus disease: France (213 cases) and Italy (one case). Back to threats overview Page contents Overview Since the beginning of 2026 and as at 11 November 2026, two countries in Europe have reported locally acquired cases of chikungunya virus disease: France (213 cases) and Italy (1 case). Since last week’s update, France has reported six new locally acquired chikungunya virus disease cases, bringing the total to 213. These cases are distributed across 31 clusters, with one additional cluster reported in the Seine-Saint-Denis department. However, this newly identified cluster is already considered closed. Fourteen clusters are currently active, including the two largest clusters in Prayssac (38 cases, Lot department) and Cavalaire-sur-Mer (34 cases, Var department). No new locally acquired chikungunya virus disease cases have been reported by Italy since last week’s update. To date, Italy has reported one locally acquired case of chikungunya virus disease in Rome. The associated cluster is closed. On 4 November, the health authorities of the Swiss Canton of Ticino, in coordination with the Swiss federal authorities, announced four locally acquired cases of chikungunya virus disease in the Piana di Magadino area of Ticino, Switzerland. These are the first locally acquired chikungunya virus disease cases ever reported in the country. As Switzerland does not report data to ECDC, these cases are not included in the map and table below. The latest weekly seasonal surveillance report showing geographical areas with locally acquired cases is available on﻿line. ECDC assessment The current chikungunya virus disease risk assessment for mainland EU/EEA is available on ECDC’s dedicated chikungunya virus disease webpage. Actions ECDC will continue to monitor the situation and will publish a weekly report on the occurrence of chikungunya virus disease cases in the EU/EEA until November 2026. Event

Scale, impact and risk
213 cases in France, 1 case in Italy, and 4 cases in Switzerland (not included in ECDC data). 31 clusters, with 14 active clusters (N1, N2, N4, N6, N7, N8, N10, N11, N12).
Response, status and uncertainty
ECDC continues to monitor the situation and publish weekly reports until November 2026. The newly identified cluster in Switzerland is considered closed (N13, N15, N5, N9).

Chikungunya Market Expected to Reach $1 Billion by 2030 Driven by Robust Industry Expansion - EIN Presswire

Chikungunya Market Expected to Reach $1 Billion by 2030 Driven by Robust Industry Expansion - EIN Presswire
新闻简报The chikungunya market is projected to grow significantly, reaching $1 billion by 2030, driven by factors such as increased investments in vaccine development, greater emphasis on preventive healthcare strategies, and expansion of vector control initiatives. The report highlights the rising global challenge of mosquito-borne diseases, including chikungunya, dengue, and Zika virus, which are transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes. The market size is expected to increase from $0.65 billion in 2025 to $0.71 billion in 2026, reflecting a compound annual growth rate (CAGR) of 9.4%. By 2030, the CAGR is projected to be 9.1%. The report notes that the Asia-Pacific region is expected to experience the fastest growth during the forecast period, while North America held the largest share in 2025. This forecast is based on industry expansion and public health trends, not confirmed outbreaks or cases. The analysis is derived from a market report by TBRC Business Research Pvt Ltd, published on November 11, 2026, by EIN Presswire.

Chikungunya Market Size and Growth Forecast Through 2026 The chikungunya market has shown robust expansion in recent years. It is projected to increase from $0.65 billion in 2025 to $0.71 billion in 2026, reflecting a compound annual growth rate (CAGR) of 9.4%. This growth during the historical period can be linked to the rising incidence of mosquito-borne viral infections, the widening of endemic areas caused by climate variability, a limited number of approved vaccines, reliance on symptomatic treatments, and enhanced public health surveillance efforts. Download a free sample of the chikungunya market report: https://thebusinessresearchcompany.com/sample_request?id=52694877&type=smp&name=Chikungunya%20Market%20Report%202026&utm_source=EINPresswire&utm_medium=Paid&utm_campaign=Nov_PR Looking ahead, the chikungunya market is expected to continue its strong upward trajectory. By 2030, the market size is predicted to reach $1 billion, growing at a CAGR of 9.1%. Factors contributing to this forecasted growth include increased investments in vaccine development, greater emphasis on preventive healthcare strategies, expansion of vector control initiatives, rising demand for rapid diagnostic tools, and a boost in global funding targeting emerging infectious diseases. Key trends anticipated in this period involve advancements in chikungunya vaccine candidates, wider adoption of antiviral therapies, heightened focus on early diagnosis, development of supportive care options, and improved mosquito-borne disease monitoring systems. Understanding Chikungunya and Its Impact Chikungunya (CHIKV) is a viral disease caused by the chikungunya virus, which is mainly spread to humans through the bites of infected Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes. The illness typically manifests with symptoms such as fever, severe joint pain, headaches, rash, and muscle aches. These symptoms can significantly disrupt the quality of life for those infected, underscoring the need for effective management and treatment solutions. View the full chikungunya market report: https://thebusinessresearchcompany.com/report/chikungunya-global-market-report?utm_source=EINPresswire&utm_medium=Paid&utm_campaign=Nov_PR Factors Fueling Growth in the Chikungunya Market The growing prevalence of mosquito-borne diseases is a major factor driving the chikungunya market forward. Diseases like chikungunya, dengue, and Zika virus, all transmitted by infected Aedes mosquitoes, constitute a serious global public health concern. The increasing frequency of these infections is influenced by climate change, rapid urbanization, deforestation, the accumulation of stagnant water, insufficient vector control measures, rising international travel, and resistance to insecticides. In addition, chikungunya treatments play a crucial role by providing symptomatic relief such as fever reduction, joint pain management, and inflammation control. These interventions help improve patient comfort, reduce complications, and speed recovery through hydration, pain relief, and supportive care. For example, in December 2024, the UK Health Security Agency reported a steep rise in dengue cases among travelers returning to England, Wales, and Northern Ireland, with 473 cases recorded from January to June 2024 — a 201% increase compared to the 157 cases during the same period in 2023. This demonstrates the growing global challenge posed by mosquito-borne diseases and highlights why the chikungunya market is expanding. Regional Overview of the Chikungunya Market In 2025, North America held the largest share of the global chikungunya market. However, the Asia-Pacific region is expected to experience the fastest growth during the forecast period. NCDC 确认两个州出现登革热,索科托出现基孔肯雅热 - 尼日利亚卫报新闻

NCDC confirms dengue in two states, chikungunya in Sokoto - The Guardian Nigeria News
新闻简报尼日利亚疾病控制和预防中心 (NCDC) 于 2026 年 11 月 11 日在索科托州和扎姆法拉州确认了登革热,在索科托州确认了基孔肯雅热。该国埃博拉病毒 暴发 的指示患者仍然呈阳性,并在瑞士住院,有 62 名接触者正在接受监测。国家疾病预防控制中心已部署包括昆虫学家在内的国家快速反应小组,以支持受影响的州进行病例发现、实验室调查和蚊子控制。总干事在一次网络研讨会上通过该机构公共卫生总监 Odianosan Ehiakhamen 博士介绍了埃博拉病毒的最新情况。国家疾病预防控制中心官员承认在识别感染方面存在延误,并指出该患者最初接受了疟疾和其他发热性疾病的治疗。国家疾病预防控制中心负责人警告说,登革热和基孔肯雅热可能会出现发烧、头痛、身体疼痛和关节疼痛等症状,敦促尼日利亚人寻求医疗评估,而不是自动将每次发烧都视为疟疾。这一进展正值尼日利亚临近旱季,旱季是脑脊髓膜炎和季节性拉沙热风险增加的时期,促使人们呼吁加强疾病 传播、早期发现和准备。
查看新闻五要素
时间
尼日利亚疾病控制和预防中心 (NCDC) 于 2026 年 11 月 11 日在两个州确诊登革热,在索科托确诊基孔肯雅热。该国埃博拉病毒 暴发 的指示患者仍然呈阳性,并在瑞士住院,有 62 名接触者正在接受监测。这一进展正值尼日利亚临近旱季,旱季是脑脊髓膜炎和季节性拉沙热风险增加的时期。总干事在 2026 年 11 月 11 日的网络研讨会上通过该机构公共卫生总监 Odianosan Ehiakhamen 博士介绍了埃博拉病毒的最新情况。
地点与对象
国家疾病预防控制中心在索科托州和扎姆法拉州确认了登革热,在索科托州确认了基孔肯雅热。国家疾病预防控制中心官员承认在识别感染方面存在延误,并指出该患者最初接受了疟疾和其他发热性疾病的治疗。

NCDC confirms dengue in two states, chikungunya in Sokoto - The Guardian Nigeria News

News Brief
The Nigeria Centre for Disease Control and Prevention (NCDC) confirmed dengue fever in Sokoto and Zamfara states, and chikungunya in Sokoto on 11 November 2026. The index patient in the country’s Ebola outbreak remains positive and hospitalised in Switzerland, with 62 contacts being monitored. The NCDC has deployed National Rapid Response Teams, including entomologists, to support affected states with case finding, laboratory investigations, and mosquito control. The DG gave the update on Ebola through the agency’s Director of Public Health, Dr Odianosan Ehiakhamen, during a webinar. The NCDC official acknowledged delays in identifying the infection, noting that the patient had initially been treated for malaria and other febrile illnesses. The NCDC chief warned that dengue and chikungunya could present with symptoms such as fever, headache, body pains, and joint pains, urging Nigerians to seek medical assessment rather than automatically treating every fever as malaria. The development comes as Nigeria approaches the dry season, a period associated with increased risk of cerebrospinal meningitis and seasonal transmission of Lassa fever, prompting calls for strengthened disease surveillance, early detection, and preparedness.

Subscribe Dark mode Home / News / NCDC confirms dengue in two states, chikungunya in Sokoto News NCDC confirms dengue in two states, chikungunya in Sokoto The Nigeria Centre for Disease Control and Prevention (NCDC) has confirmed dengue fever in Sokoto and Zamfara states, and chikungunya Dengue fever Ijeoma Nwanosike 11 November 2026 3 min read Get Guardian on Google Ebola index patient remains positive The Nigeria Centre for Disease Control and Prevention (NCDC) has confirmed dengue fever in Sokoto and Zamfara states, and chikungunya in Sokoto, even as health authorities brace for the seasonal resurgence of other potentially deadly infections. NCDC also disclosed that the index patient in the country’s Ebola outbreak remains positive and hospitalised in Switzerland, while 62 contacts were being monitored. The development comes as Nigeria approaches the dry season, a period associated with increased risk of cerebrospinal meningitis and seasonal transmission of Lassa fever, prompting calls for strengthened disease surveillance, early detection and preparedness. The agency urged Nigerians to remain vigilant against multiple infectious diseases, warning against assuming that every fever is malaria, as it disclosed that the country had recorded no additional Ebola case three weeks after confirming an imported infection caused by the Bundibugyo virus. Director-General of NCDC, Dr Jide Idris, disclosed this in a national health security update/advisory yesterday, marking 21 days since Nigeria confirmed the imported Ebola case on October 20, 2026. Idris said the agency had deployed National Rapid Response Teams, including entomologists from the National Arbovirus Research Centre, Enugu, to support affected states with case finding, laboratory investigations, clinical readiness, community engagement and mosquito control. He confirmed that the teams were working with state authorities to address gaps identified during field investigations and monitor the evolving disease situation. The NCDC chief explained that dengue and chikungunya could present with symptoms such as fever, headache, body pains and joint pains, making it important for Nigerians to seek medical assessment rather than automatically treating every fever as malaria. Chikungunya is a mosquito-borne viral infection caused by the chikungunya virus (CHIKV), characterised by fever, severe joint pain and other flu-like symptoms, while dengue is caused by the dengue virus and presents with high fever, severe headache, muscle and joint pain, nausea and skin rash, with severe cases potentially resulting in life-threatening complications. He warned that dengue could become life-threatening when warning signs were missed, and advised people with persistent fever, particularly when accompanied by rash, vomiting or severe joint pains, to seek medical attention. The DG gave the update on Ebola through the agency’s Director of Public Health, Dr Odianosan Ehiakhamen, during a Nigerian Institute of Medical Research (NIMR) webinar entitled ‘Safeguarding Nigeria Together: The NIMR Ebola Webinar’. Ehiakhamen said the patient’s viral load appeared to be declining, as indicated by rising cycle threshold (Ct) values, although the test results remained positive. He added that the patient’s spouse tested negative and was being accommodated separately. Six contacts, he added, had been discharged from quarantine, while the agency was targeting December 7 for a possible declaration of the end of the outbreak, provided no new case emerged and all monitoring requirements were met. The NCDC official acknowledged delays in identifying the infection, noting that the patient had initially been treated for malaria and other febrile illnesses. He identified early detection, healthcare workers’ awareness, the availability of isolation facilities and coordination between federal and state authorities as areas requiring improvement. Meanwhile, a Professor of Community Medicine at the Lagos State University College of Medicine (LASUCOM) and Lagos University Teaching Hospital (LUTH), Prof. Lolade Wright, warned that Nigeria risked further transmission without dedicated funding for infection prevention and control (IPC). Wright called for dedicated IPC budget lines at the federal and state levels, rather than continued reliance on emergency funding during outbreaks. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

基孔肯雅热病例突破 1,000 例,哥斯达黎加登革热发病率上升 58% - Tico Times

Dengue Up 58% in Costa Rica as Chikungunya Passes 1,000 Cases - The Tico Times
新闻简报2026年11月,哥斯达黎加报告登革热病例大幅增加,较2025年同期增加58%,确诊病例达6,594例。基孔肯雅热已经消失了近十年,到 10 月中旬病例已超过 1,000 例,其中确诊病例 916 例,疑似病例 133 例。暴发集中在瓜纳卡斯特,其中乔罗特加卫生区占全国总数的57%。阿拉胡埃拉省的登革热病例数最多,为 2,791 例,其次是蓬塔雷纳斯省和圣何塞省。卫生部和哥斯达黎加社会保障系统(CCSS)正在追踪疫情的传播,美国疾病控制与预防中心指出登革热风险高于预期。公共卫生措施包括使用驱虫剂、穿长袖衣服和消除蚊子孳生的水源。旱季已经开始,但储存的水仍然会滋生蚊子,因此容器应盖好。由于可能出现严重病例并且需要持续保持警惕,暴发 值得关注。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026年11月11日。2026年登革热病例与去年同期相比增加了58%。到今年 10 月中旬的第 42 个流行病学周,基孔肯雅热病例达到 1,049 例。
地点与对象
暴发主要位于瓜纳卡斯特,其中乔罗特加卫生区占全国总数的57%。阿拉胡埃拉省的登革热病例最多,为 2,791 例,其次是蓬塔雷纳斯省,有 981 例,圣何塞有 880 例。拉克鲁斯、卡里略和圣克鲁斯等其他地区也报告了基孔肯雅热病例。
事件
2026年,哥斯达黎加的登革热病例增加了58%,近十年来未出现的基孔肯雅热病例已超过1000例。卫生部报告了 6,594 例登革热病例,其中 105 例被列为有警告迹象的登革热病例。基孔肯雅热病例包括 916 例经实验室检测确诊的病例和 133 例疑似病例仍在研究中。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年,登革热病例达到 6,594 例,其中 105 例为重症。基孔肯雅热病例总数为 1,049 例,其中确诊 916 例,疑似病例 133 例。卫生部和哥斯达黎加社会保障系统(CCSS)报告了这些数字。美国疾病控制与预防中心将病例上升标记为登革热风险高于预期。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部和 CCSS 正在监测 暴发。公共卫生措施包括使用驱虫剂、穿长袖衣服和消除蚊子孳生的水源。文章提到,当地的 传播 于 7 月在瓜纳卡斯特和阿拉胡埃拉首次得到确认。旱季已经开始,但水箱和水桶中储存的水仍然会滋生蚊子。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Dengue Up 58% in Costa Rica as Chikungunya Passes 1,000 Cases - The Tico Times

News Brief
In November 2026, Costa Rica reported a significant rise in dengue cases, up 58% compared to the same period in 2025, with 6,594 confirmed cases. Chikungunya, which had been absent for nearly a decade, surpassed 1,000 cases by mid-October, with 916 confirmed and 133 probable cases. The outbreak is concentrated in Guanacaste, where the Chorotega health region accounts for 57% of the national total. Alajuela province has the highest number of dengue cases at 2,791, followed by Puntarenas and San José. The Ministry of Health and the Costa Rican Social Security System (CCSS) are tracking the spread, and the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention noted higher-than-expected dengue risk. Public health measures include using repellent, wearing long sleeves, and eliminating water sources for mosquito breeding. The dry season has begun, but stored water can still breed mosquitoes, so containers should be kept covered. The outbreak is a concern due to the potential for severe cases and the need for continued vigilance.
Original source text (English)

Most of the chikungunya cases are in Guanacaste. The Ministry of Health has counted 6,594 dengue cases so far in 2026, compared with 4,181 at the same point last year. Of those, 105 were classified as dengue with warning signs, the stage that can turn severe. Costa Rica Videos CC SettingsOffArabicChineseEnglishFrenchGermanHindiPortugueseSpanish Font ColorwhiteFont Opacity100%Font Size100%Font FamilyArialText ShadownoneBackground ColorblackBackground Opacity50%Window ColorblackWindow Opacity0% WhiteBlackRedGreenBlueYellowMagentaCyan 100%75%50%25% 200%175%150%125%100%75%50% ArialGeorgiaGaramondCourier NewTahomaTimes New RomanTrebuchet MSVerdana NoneRaisedDepressedUniformDrop Shadow WhiteBlackRedGreenBlueYellowMagentaCyan 100%75%50%25%0% WhiteBlackRedGreenBlueYellowMagentaCyan 100%75%50%25%0% Video Muted As of Nov. 2, three patients were hospitalized with dengue, according to the Costa Rican Social Security System (CCSS). The rise follows a September notice in which the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention flagged Costa Rica for higher-than-expected dengue risk. Alajuela province has the most dengue cases at 2,791, followed by Puntarenas with 981 and San José with 880. By county, Grecia leads with 727, then Alajuela with 658, Orotina with 591, San José with 418 and Puntarenas with 416. Orotina stands out. It has 2,323.8 cases per 100,000 people, about 18 times the national rate. The town sits on Route 27, the main highway from San José to the Central Pacific beaches. Chikungunya reached 1,049 cases through the 42nd epidemiological week of the year, in mid-October. Of those, 916 were confirmed by lab tests and 133 are probable cases still under study, an increase of 126 in two weeks. The Chorotega health region, which covers Guanacaste, has 602 chikungunya cases, or 57% of the national total. La Cruz, near the Nicaraguan border, has 389, Carrillo has 90 and Santa Cruz has 64. Outside Guanacaste, Alajuela has 136 and San José has 71. Local transmission was first confirmed in July in a Guanacaste beach town and in Alajuela. In September, the virus reached Pavas, on the west side of San José. Both diseases spread through mosquito bites, so the best protection is not getting bitten. Use repellent, especially during the day, when the mosquito that carries them is most active, and wear long sleeves when you can. Empty anything around your home that holds water, such as plant saucers, buckets, old tires and clogged gutters. That is where these mosquitoes lay their eggs. If you get a fever, headache or body aches, see a doctor. Don’t take ibuprofen or aspirin until dengue has been ruled out, because these drugs can raise the risk of bleeding if you have dengue. Get care right away if you notice intense abdominal pain, repeated vomiting, bleeding from your gums or nose, or extreme weakness. These are signs that dengue is turning severe. The dry season, which began this month in Guanacaste and the Central Valley, means fewer puddles. Water stored in tanks and buckets during the dry months can still breed mosquitoes, so keep containers covered. 卫生部确认古巴面临广泛传播的登革热和基孔肯雅热 暴发 - CubaHeadlines

Cuba Faces Widespread Dengue and Chikungunya Outbreak, Health Ministry Confirms - CubaHeadlines
新闻简报古巴卫生部确认了登革热和基孔肯雅热 暴发 的广泛传播,报告称 基孔肯雅病毒 的存在是其病因。暴发 于 2026 年 11 月 16 日报告,但证据中未提供具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露数量。这一确认是在有关该国健康挑战的更广泛讨论之际作出的,但没有具体说明应对措施的细节,例如调查、测试或预防措施。该报告强调了与 基孔肯雅病毒 相关的持续公共卫生问题,基孔肯雅病毒 是主题合同下分析的主要候选者。然而,证据并未包括有关 暴发 规模或解决该问题所采取行动的任何进一步细节。

时间

事件
古巴卫生部确认了登革热和基孔肯雅热 暴发 的广泛传播,报告称 基孔肯雅病毒 的存在是其病因。
规模、影响与风险
暴发 被描述为广泛传播,但所提供的证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡数或暴露数量。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部确认了暴发,但证据中没有提供有关调查、检测、隔离、接触者追踪或预防措施的详细信息。

Cuba Faces Widespread Dengue and Chikungunya Outbreak, Health Ministry Confirms - CubaHeadlines

News Brief
Cuba's Health Ministry confirmed a widespread outbreak of dengue and chikungunya, with the report citing the presence of the chikungunya virus as the cause. The outbreak was reported on 2026-11-16, but no specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers are provided in the evidence. The confirmation comes amid broader discussions about the country's health challenges, though the details of the response, such as investigation, testing, or prevention measures, are not specified. The report highlights the ongoing public health concerns related to the chikungunya virus, which is a primary candidate for analysis under the topic contract. However, the evidence does not include any further details on the scale of the outbreak or the actions taken to address it.
